SOURCE_JSON {"worktree": "fastwam-current-grpo", "rel": "rlinf/algorithms/advantages.py", "sha256": "a324ac98794fe9fcb1d2df3186ef1865c03cd2b06029aa3b4ffc03ab2059e791", "text": "# Copyright 2025 The RLinf Authors.\n#\n# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n# you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n# You may obtain a copy of the License at\n#\n#     https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n#\n# Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n# distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n# WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n# limitations under the License.\n\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nimport torch\n\nfrom rlinf.algorithms.registry import register_advantage\nfrom rlinf.algorithms.utils import kl_penalty, safe_normalize\nfrom rlinf.utils.utils import masked_mean\n\n\n@register_advantage(\"gae\")\ndef compute_gae_advantages_and_returns(\n    rewards: torch.Tensor,\n    gamma: float = 1.0,\n    gae_lambda: float = 1.0,\n    values: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n    normalize_advantages: bool = True,\n    normalize_returns: bool = False,\n    loss_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n    dones: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n    **kwargs,\n) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate advantages and returns for Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO).\n    NOTE: currently this function does not support auto-reset.\n\n    This function implements Generalized Advantage Estimation (GAE) to compute\n    advantages and returns for PPO training. The advantages are normalized\n    using mean and standard deviation for stable training.\n\n    Args:\n        rewards (torch.Tensor): Rewards per timestep. Shape: [seq_len, bsz].\n        values (torch.Tensor): Value function estimates. Shape: [seq_len, bsz].\n        dones (torch.Tensor): Done flags (1 if episode ended, else 0).\n        gamma (float, optional): Discount factor. Defaults to 1.0.\n        gae_lambda (float, optional): GAE smoothing factor. Defaults to 1.0.\n        normalize_advantages (bool, optional): Whether to normalize advantages. Defaults to True.\n        normalize_returns (bool, optional): Whether to normalize returns. Defaults to False.\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]: (advantages, returns)\n    \"\"\"\n    T = rewards.shape[0]\n    advantages = torch.zeros_like(rewards)\n    returns = torch.zeros_like(rewards)\n    gae = 0\n\n    critic_free = values is None\n    if critic_free:\n        gae_lambda = 1\n        gamma = 1\n\n    for step in reversed(range(T)):\n        if critic_free:\n            delta = rewards[step]\n        else:\n            delta = (\n                rewards[step]\n                + gamma * values[step + 1] * (~dones[step + 1])\n                - values[step]\n            )\n\n        gae = delta + gamma * gae_lambda * (~dones[step + 1]) * gae\n        returns[step] = gae if critic_free else gae + values[step]\n\n    advantages = returns - values[:-1] if not critic_free else returns\n\n    if normalize_advantages:\n        advantages = safe_normalize(advantages, loss_mask=loss_mask)\n    if normalize_returns:\n        returns = safe_normalize(returns, loss_mask=loss_mask)\n\n    return advantages, returns\n\n\n@register_advantage(\"grpo\")\ndef compute_grpo_advantages(\n    rewards: torch.Tensor,\n    loss_mask: torch.Tensor,\n    group_size: int,\n    **kwargs,\n):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute GRPO advantages.\n\n    Args:\n        rewards (torch.Tensor): Reward or score values. Shape: [num_groups, group_size]\n        loss_mask (torch.Tensor): Loss mask for valid entries. Shape: [num_groups, group_size]\n        group_size (int): Group size for advantage computation.\n\n    Returns:\n        torch.Tensor: advantages\n    \"\"\"\n    grouped_rewards = rewards.view(-1, group_size)\n\n    grouped_reward_mean = grouped_rewards.mean(dim=-1, keepdim=True).expand_as(\n        grouped_rewards\n    )\n    grouped_reward_std = grouped_rewards.std(dim=-1, keepdim=True).expand_as(\n        grouped_rewards\n    )\n\n    advantages = grouped_rewards - grouped_reward_mean\n    advantages = advantages / (grouped_reward_std + 1e-6)\n\n    advantages = (torch.zeros_like(loss_mask) + advantages.view(1, -1)) * loss_mask\n\n    return advantages, None\n\n\n@register_advantage(\"grpo_video\")\ndef compute_grpo_video_advantages(\n    rewards: torch.Tensor,\n    loss_mask: torch.Tensor,\n    group_size: int,\n    **kwargs,\n):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute fine-grained GRPO advantages for video frame/chunk rewards.\n\n    After embodied preprocessing, action-level video rewards have shape\n    [num_steps, batch_size], where num_steps is usually the number of decoded\n    frame chunks and batch_size is ordered as [num_prompts * group_size]. This\n    function reshapes rewards to [num_steps, num_prompts, group_size] and returns\n    advantages with the same [num_steps, batch_size] shape, so each frame/chunk\n    can receive its own credit signal.\n\n    advantage_mode=\"frame\":\n        Normalize each frame/chunk independently over the group_size samples.\n        Frame 0 only competes with frame 0 from other samples of the same prompt;\n        frame 1 only competes with frame 1, and so on.\n\n    advantage_mode=\"video\":\n        Normalize all frame/chunk rewards within the same prompt group together\n        over dimensions [num_steps, group_size]. This keeps frame-level\n        advantages but uses one video-level mean/std per prompt group.\n    \"\"\"\n    num_steps, batch_size = rewards.shape\n    grouped_rewards = rewards.reshape(num_steps, -1, group_size)\n    advantage_mode = kwargs.get(\"advantage_mode\")\n    if advantage_mode == \"frame\":\n        grouped_reward_mean = grouped_rewards.mean(dim=-1, keepdim=True)\n        grouped_reward_std = grouped_rewards.std(dim=-1, keepdim=True)\n    elif advantage_mode == \"video\":\n        grouped_reward_mean = grouped_rewards.mean(dim=(0, 2), keepdim=True)\n        grouped_reward_std = grouped_rewards.std(dim=(0, 2), keepdim=True)\n    else:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Unsupported grpo_video advantage_mode: {advantage_mode}\")\n    advantages = (grouped_rewards - grouped_reward_mean) / (grouped_reward_std + 1e-6)\n    advantages = advantages.reshape(num_steps, batch_size)\n    return advantages * loss_mask, None\n\n\n@register_advantage(\"grpo_dynamic\")\ndef compute_grpo_dynamic_advantages(\n    rewards: torch.Tensor,\n    loss_mask: torch.Tensor,\n    group_size: int,\n    idx_to_traj: list[int],\n    advantage_mode: str = \"turn\",  # \"trajectory\" or \"turn\"\n    **kwargs,\n):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute GRPO advantages for multi-turn multi-agent scenarios.\n\n    IMPORTANT: This function computes advantages PER QUESTION, not globally.\n    - idx_to_traj maps turn_idx -> global_traj_idx (e.g., [0,0,1,1,2,2,3,3,4,4,...,15,15])\n    - Trajectories 0-3 belong to question 0, 4-7 to question 1, etc.\n    - We must compute GRPO separately for each question's group_size trajectories\n\n    Two advantage computation modes:\n    1. \"trajectory\": Trajectory-level GRPO (Method 1)\n       - Compute mean/std over group_size trajectory rewards per question\n       - Broadcast same advantage to all turns in a trajectory\n       - Example: Q0 has 4 trajs with 1,2,3,4 turns. Compute GRPO over 4 traj rewards,\n                  then assign traj0_adv to its 1 turn, traj1_adv to its 2 turns, etc.\n\n    2. \"turn\": Turn-level GRPO (Method 2)\n       - Compute mean/std over all turns within each question\n       - Example: Q0 has 4 trajs with 1,2,3,4 turns = 10 turns total.\n                  Compute GRPO over these 10 turn rewards (currently all same within traj).\n       - Future-proof: works when turns have different rewards within same trajectory\n\n    Args:\n        rewards: Shape [num_sequence, 1] after preprocessing (num_sequence = total turns)\n        loss_mask: Shape [seq_len, num_sequence] after preprocessing\n        group_size: Number of trajectories per question (e.g., 4)\n        idx_to_traj: List mapping turn_idx -> global_traj_idx\n        advantage_mode: \"trajectory\" or \"turn\"\n\n    Returns:\n        advantages: Shape [seq_len, num_sequence]\n    \"\"\"\n    num_sequence = len(idx_to_traj)\n\n    rewards_flat = rewards.squeeze(-1)\n\n    assert rewards_flat.numel() == num_sequence, (\n        f\"Rewards size mismatch: {rewards_flat.numel()} != {num_sequence}\"\n    )\n\n    num_trajectories = max(idx_to_traj) + 1\n    num_questions = num_trajectories // group_size\n    assert num_trajectories % group_size == 0, (\n        f\"num_trajectories {num_trajectories} not divisible by group_size {group_size}\"\n    )\n\n    turn_advantages = torch.zeros(\n        num_sequence, dtype=rewards.dtype, device=rewards.device\n    )\n\n    if advantage_mode == \"trajectory\":\n        # Aggregate turn rewards into per-trajectory rewards first.\n        trajectory_rewards = torch.zeros(\n            num_trajectories, dtype=rewards.dtype, device=rewards.device\n        )\n        trajectory_counts = torch.zeros(\n            num_trajectories, dtype=torch.long, device=rewards.device\n        )\n\n        for turn_idx, traj_idx in enumerate(idx_to_traj):\n            trajectory_rewards[traj_idx] += rewards_flat[turn_idx]\n            trajectory_counts[traj_idx] += 1\n\n        # Step 1: Average rewards per trajectory.\n        trajectory_rewards = trajectory_rewards / trajectory_counts.clamp(min=1).float()\n\n        # Step 2: reshape to [num_questions, group_size] for per-question GRPO.\n        trajectory_rewards_grouped = trajectory_rewards.view(num_questions, group_size)\n\n        # Step 3: compute per-question mean and std.\n        per_question_mean = trajectory_rewards_grouped.mean(\n            dim=-1, keepdim=True\n        )  # [num_questions, 1]\n        per_question_std = trajectory_rewards_grouped.std(\n            dim=-1, keepdim=True\n        )  # [num_questions, 1]\n\n        # Step 4: normalize within each question group.\n        normalized_trajectory_rewards = (\n            trajectory_rewards_grouped - per_question_mean\n        ) / (per_question_std + 1e-6)  # [num_questions, group_size]\n\n        # Step 5: flatten back to [num_trajectories].\n        normalized_trajectory_rewards = normalized_trajectory_rewards.view(-1)\n\n        # Step 6: broadcast trajectory advantages to all turns in that trajectory.\n        for turn_idx, traj_idx in enumerate(idx_to_traj):\n            turn_advantages[turn_idx] = normalized_trajectory_rewards[traj_idx]\n\n    elif advantage_mode == \"turn\":\n        # Step 1: map each turn to its owning question.\n        turn_to_question = torch.tensor(\n            [idx_to_traj[i] // group_size for i in range(num_sequence)],\n            dtype=torch.long,\n            device=rewards.device,\n        )\n\n        # Step 2: normalize turn rewards within each question group.\n        for question_idx in range(num_questions):\n            question_mask = turn_to_question == question_idx\n            question_turn_rewards = rewards_flat[question_mask]\n\n            # Step 3: compute mean and std for all turns in this question.\n            question_mean = question_turn_rewards.mean()\n            question_std = question_turn_rewards.std()\n\n            # Step 4: normalize turn rewards within the question.\n            normalized_question_rewards = (question_turn_rewards - question_mean) / (\n                question_std + 1e-6\n            )\n\n            # Step 5: write normalized turn-level advantages back.\n            turn_advantages[question_mask] = normalized_question_rewards\n\n    else:\n        raise ValueError(\n            f\"Invalid advantage_mode: {advantage_mode}. Must be 'trajectory' or 'turn'\"\n        )\n\n    advantages = torch.zeros_like(\n        loss_mask, dtype=rewards.dtype\n    ) + turn_advantages.view(1, -1)\n    advantages = advantages * loss_mask\n\n    return advantages, None\n\n\n@register_advantage(\"reinpp\")\ndef compute_reinpp_advantages(\n    rewards: torch.Tensor,\n    loss_mask: torch.Tensor,\n    group_size: int,\n    use_reinpp_baseline: bool = False,\n    kl_beta: float = 0.0,\n    logprob=None,\n    ref_logprob=None,\n    kl_penalty_type: str = \"\",\n    **kwargs,\n):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute advantages for reinforce++ and reinforce++ baseline.\n\n    Args:\n        rewards (torch.Tensor): The reward or score values.\n        loss_mask (torch.Tensor): The loss mask for valid entries.\n        group_size (int): The group size for advantage computation.\n        use_reinpp_baseline (bool, optional): Whether to use reinforce++ baseline.\n        kl_beta (float, optional): KL penalty coefficient.\n        logprob (optional): Log probability of current policy.\n        ref_logprob (optional): Log probability of reference policy.\n        kl_penalty_type (str, optional): Type of KL penalty.\n\n    Returns:\n        torch.Tensor: advantages\n    \"\"\"\n    # first group baseline for reinforce++ baseline\n    if use_reinpp_baseline:\n        grouped_rewards = rewards.view(-1, group_size)  # [num_prompt, group_size]\n        grouped_rewards -= grouped_rewards.mean(dim=1, keepdims=True)\n        rewards = grouped_rewards.view(-1)  # [B]\n\n    # build the reward matrix\n    r_matrix = torch.zeros_like(loss_mask).float()  # [L, B]\n    seq_length = loss_mask.size(0)\n    mask_flipped = loss_mask.long().fliplr()\n    eos_positions = mask_flipped.argmax(\n        dim=0, keepdim=True\n    )  # position of last True in original mask\n    eos_indices = seq_length - 1 - eos_positions  # [1, B]\n\n    r_matrix = r_matrix.scatter_(dim=0, index=eos_indices, src=rewards)  # [L, B]\n\n    # add kl penalty\n    if kl_beta > 0:\n        kld = kl_penalty(logprob, ref_logprob, kl_penalty=kl_penalty_type)  # [L, B]\n        r_matrix -= kl_beta * kld\n\n    # compute return\n    ret_matrix = torch.cumsum(r_matrix.flip(dims=[0]), dim=0).flip(dims=[0])\n\n    # normalize\n    advantages = ret_matrix.clone()\n\n    mean = masked_mean(advantages, loss_mask)\n    var = masked_mean((advantages - mean).pow(2), loss_mask)\n    rstd = var.clamp(min=1e-8).rsqrt()\n\n    advantages = (advantages - mean) * rstd\n\n    return advantages, None\n\n\n@register_advantage(\"opd\")\ndef compute_opd_advantages(\n    prev_logprobs: torch.Tensor,\n    teacher_logprobs: torch.Tensor,\n    loss_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n    normalize_advantages: bool = False,\n    **kwargs,\n):\n    \"\"\"Compute OPD advantages from frozen teacher token log-probabilities.\"\"\"\n    assert teacher_logprobs is not None, (\n        \"OPD advantage computation requires post-rollout teacher_logprobs.\"\n    )\n    assert prev_logprobs is not None, (\n        \"OPD advantage computation requires prev_logprobs from student rollout.\"\n    )\n    assert teacher_logprobs.shape == prev_logprobs.shape, (\n        f\"teacher_logprobs shape {teacher_logprobs.shape} must match \"\n        f\"prev_logprobs shape {prev_logprobs.shape}.\"\n    )\n    assert not normalize_advantages, (\n        \"VLA-OPD uses raw reverse-KL rewards; set normalize_advantages to False.\"\n    )\n    num_action_chunks = kwargs.get(\"num_action_chunks\", None)\n    assert num_action_chunks is not None, (\n        \"OPD advantage computation requires num_action_chunks.\"\n    )\n    advantages = teacher_logprobs.float() - prev_logprobs.float()\n    assert advantages.shape[-1] % num_action_chunks == 0, (\n        f\"OPD token count {advantages.shape[-1]} must be divisible by \"\n        f\"num_action_chunks {num_action_chunks}.\"\n    )\n    advantages = advantages.reshape(*advantages.shape[:-1], num_action_chunks, -1)\n    if loss_mask is not None:\n        target_steps = loss_mask.shape[0]\n        assert advantages.shape[0] in {target_steps, target_steps + 1}, (\n            f\"OPD advantages time dimension {advantages.shape[0]} must match \"\n            f\"loss_mask time dimension {target_steps} or include one bootstrap step.\"\n        )\n        advantages = advantages[:target_steps]\n\n    return advantages, None\n\n\n@register_advantage(\"raw\")\ndef compute_raw_advantages(\n    rewards: torch.Tensor,\n    loss_mask: torch.Tensor,\n    normalize_advantages: bool = False,\n    **kwargs,\n):\n    \"\"\"\n    Return raw rewards or normalized rewards.\n\n    Args:\n        rewards (torch.Tensor): Reward or score values. Shape: [num_groups, group_size]\n        loss_mask (torch.Tensor): Loss mask for valid entries. Shape: [num_groups, group_size]\n        normalize_advantages (bool): Whether to normalize advantages.\n\n    Returns:\n        torch.Tensor: advantages\n    \"\"\"\n    if rewards.ndim == 2:\n        rewards = rewards.reshape(-1)\n    advantages = rewards.unsqueeze(0).expand_as(loss_mask) * loss_mask\n\n    # Simple baseline subtraction (mean of valid advantages)\n    if normalize_advantages:\n        valid = advantages[loss_mask.bool()]\n        if valid.numel() > 0:\n            advantages = (advantages - valid.mean()) / (valid.std() + 1e-5)\n\n    return advantages, None\n"}

  loss_mask_ratio = (loss_mask_sum * 1.0) / max_episode_steps\n        loss_agg_func = masked_mean_ratio\n\n    if proximal_logprobs is None:\n        if versions is None or current_version is None:\n            proximal_logprobs = old_logprobs.detach()\n        else:\n            v_behav = versions.float()\n            v_theta = float(current_version)\n            v_prox = v_theta - 1.0\n\n            version_diff = v_theta - v_behav\n            version_gap = v_prox - v_behav\n            generated_tokens_mask = versions >= 0\n            alpha = torch.where(\n                (version_diff > alpha = alpha.unsqueeze(-1)\n            alpha = torch.clamp(alpha, 0.0, 1.0)\n            proximal_logprobs = (\n                old_logprobs + alpha * (logprobs - old_logprobs)\n            ).detach()\n\n    assert proximal_logprobs.dtype == torch.float32, (\n        \"proximal_logprobs must be float32 to keep numerical stability\"\n    )\n\n    loss_mask_count = proximal_ratio, 1.0 - clip_ratio_low, 1.0 + clip_ratio_high\n    )\n\n    pg_loss1 = -advantages * proximal_ratio\n    pg_loss2 = -advantages * clipped_proximal_ratio\n    pg_loss = dual_clip_mask = pg_loss3.detach() < pg_loss.detach()\n        pg_loss = torch.min(pg_loss, pg_loss3)\n    else:\n        dual_clip_mask = torch.zeros_like(pg_loss, dtype=torch.bool)\n\n    behav_weight = torch.exp(proximal_logprobs - old_logprobs)\n    behav_mask = (\n        (behav_weight <= behave_weight_threshold).logical_and(loss_mask)\n        if behave_weight_threshold is not -torch.where(loss_mask, logprobs - proximal_logprobs, 0.0).sum()\n            / loss_mask_count\n        )\n        behav_approx_kl = (\n            -torch.where(behav_mask, proximal_logprobs - metrics_data\n\n\ndef compute_ppo_actor_loss(\n    logprobs: torch.Tensor,\n    old_logprobs: torch.Tensor,\n    clip_ratio_low: float,\n    clip_ratio_high: float,\n    advantages: torch.Tensor,\n    loss_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n    clip_ratio_c: Optional[float] = None,\n    loss_agg_func: Optional[Callable[..., torch.Tensor]] = masked_mean,\n    max_episode_steps: Optional[int] = None,\n    loss_mask_sum: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n    critic_warmup: Optional[bool] = False,\n    clip_log_ratio_min: Optional[float] = None,\n    clip_log_ratio_max: Optional[float] = None,\n    fast_path_zero_loss_mask: Optional[bool] = False,\n    **kwargs,\n) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute PPO actor loss function.\n\n    Args:\n        logprobs (torch.FloatTensor): Log probabilities of actions.\n        old_logprobs (torch.FloatTensor): Old log probabilities of actions.\n        clip_ratio_low (float): Lower bound of clipping ratio.\n        clip_ratio_high (float): Upper bound of clipping ratio.\n        advantages (torch.FloatTensor): GAE (normalized) advantages.\n        loss_mask (Optional[torch.BoolTensor], optional): Mask for valid entries. Defaults to None.\n        clip_ratio_c (Optional[float], optional): Optional clipping coefficient. Defaults to None.\n        loss_agg_func (callable, optional): Aggregation function (e.g., masked_mean). Defaults to None.\n        max_episode_steps (Optional[int], optional): Max episode length for normalization. Defaults to None.\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple[torch.Tensor, Dict]: (actor_loss, metrics_dict)\n    \"\"\"\n    if fast_path_zero_loss_mask and (\n        loss_mask is not None and loss_mask[0].sum() == 0.0\n    ):\n        return torch.tensor(0.0, device=logprobs.device), {\n            \"actor/token_num\": torch.tensor(0.0, device=logprobs.device),\n            \"actor/policy_loss\": torch.tensor(0.0, device=logprobs.device),\n            \"actor/policy_loss_mbs_mean\": torch.tensor(0.0, device=logprobs.device),\n            \"actor/policy_loss_abs\": torch.tensor(0.0, device=logprobs.device),\n            \"actor/ratio\": torch.tensor(0.0, device=logprobs.device),\n            \"actor/clipped_ratio\": torch.tensor(0.0, device=logprobs.device),\n            \"actor/dual_cliped_ratio\": torch.tensor(0.0, device=logprobs.device),\n            \"actor/approx_kl\": torch.tensor(0.0, device=logprobs.device),\n            \"actor/clip_fraction\": torch.tensor(0.0, device=logprobs.device),\n        }\n\n    loss_mask_ratio = None\n\n    if (\n        max_episode_steps is not None\n        and loss_mask_sum is not None\n        and loss_mask is not None\n    ):\n        loss_mask_ratio = (loss_mask_sum * 1.0) / max_episode_steps\n        loss_agg_func = masked_mean_ratio\n\n    if loss_mask is None:\n        loss_mask = torch.ones_like(logprobs).bool()\n\n    assert logprobs.dtype == torch.float32, (\n        \"logprobs must be float32 to keep numerical stability\"\n    )\n    assert old_logprobs.dtype == torch.float32, (\n        \"old_logprobs must be float32 to keep numerical stability\"\n    )\n    assert advantages.dtype == torch.float32, (\n        \"advantages must be float32 to keep numerical stability\"\n    )\n\n    loss_mask_count = loss_mask.count_nonzero() or 1\n    # For numerical stability.\n    log_ratio = logprobs - old_logprobs\n    if clip_log_ratio_min is not None:\n        log_ratio = torch.clamp(log_ratio, min=clip_log_ratio_min)\n    if clip_log_ratio_max is not None:\n        log_ratio = torch.clamp(log_ratio, max=clip_log_ratio_max)\n    ratio = torch.where(loss_mask, torch.exp(log_ratio), 0)\n    approx_kl = torch.where(loss_mask, log_ratio.detach(), 0.0)\n\n    clipped_ratio = torch.clamp(ratio, 1.0 - clip_ratio_low, 1.0 + clip_ratio_high)\n    policy_loss1 = -advantages * ratio\n    policy_loss2 = -advantages * clipped_ratio\n\n    clip_mask = policy_loss1.detach() < policy_loss2.detach()\n\n    policy_loss = torch.max(policy_loss1, policy_loss2)\n    if clip_ratio_c is not None:\n        assert clip_ratio_c > 1.0, \"clip_ratio_c must be greater than 1.0\"\n        policy_loss3 = torch.sign(advantages) * clip_ratio_c * advantages\n        dual_clip_mask = policy_loss3.detach() < policy_loss.detach()\n        policy_loss = torch.min(policy_loss, policy_loss3)\n    else:\n        dual_clip_mask = torch.zeros_like(clip_mask)\n\n    metric_policy_loss_abs = loss_agg_func(\n        policy_loss.abs(), loss_mask, loss_mask_ratio\n    )\n    policy_loss = loss_agg_func(\n        policy_loss, loss_mask, loss_mask_ratio\n    )  # default max_episode_steps is None\n\n    clip_mask = policy_loss1.detach() < policy_loss2.detach()\n    dual_clip_mask = (dual_clip_mask * loss_mask).bool()\n\n    clip_fraction = (clip_mask * loss_mask).sum() / float(loss_mask_count)\n    approx_kl = -torch.sum(approx_kl) / float(loss_mask_count)\n\n    dual_cliped_ratio = torch.where(dual_clip_mask, ratio, 0)\n\n    if critic_warmup:\n        policy_loss = torch.tensor(0.0, device=policy_loss.device)\n\n    # Compile metrics for logging\n    loss_mask_for_metrics = loss_mask\n    ratio_for_metrics = ratio.detach()\n    ratio_abs_for_metrics = (ratio - 1).abs().detach()\n    clipped_ratio_for_metrics = clipped_ratio.detach()\n    dual_cliped_ratio_for_metrics = dual_cliped_ratio.detach()\n\n    # Only broadcast when ratio has action_dim dimension and loss_mask's last dim is 1\n    # This handles token_level mode: ratio [bsz, num_chunks, action_dim], loss_mask [bsz, num_chunks, 1]\n    if len(ratio.shape) > 2 and loss_mask.shape[-1] == 1 and ratio.shape[-1] > 1:\n        # Broadcast loss_mask to match ratio's shape for metrics computation\n        loss_mask_for_metrics = loss_mask.expand_as(ratio)\n\n    metrics_data = {\n        \"actor/policy_loss\": policy_loss.detach(),\n        \"actor/policy_loss_abs\": metric_policy_loss_abs.detach(),\n        \"actor/ratio\": masked_mean(ratio_for_metrics, loss_mask_for_metrics),\n        \"actor/ratio_abs\": masked_mean(ratio_abs_for_metrics, loss_mask_for_metrics),\n        \"actor/clipped_ratio\": masked_mean(\n            clipped_ratio_for_metrics, loss_mask_for_metrics\n        ),\n        \"actor/dual_cliped_ratio\": masked_mean(\n            dual_cliped_ratio_for_metrics, loss_mask_for_metrics\n        ),\n        \"actor/approx_kl\": approx_kl.detach(),\n        \"actor/clip_fraction\": clip_fraction.detach(),\n    }\n    return policy_loss, metrics_data\n\n\ndef compute_ppo_critic_loss(\n    values: torch.Tensor,\n    returns: torch.Tensor,\n    prev_values: torch.Tensor,\n    value_clip: float,\n    huber_delta: float,\n    loss_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n    max_episode_steps: Optional[int] = None,\n    loss_mask_sum: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n    **kwargs,\n) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute PPO critic loss function.\n\n    Args:\n        values (torch.Tensor): Current value predictions.\n        returns (torch.Tensor): Return values.\n        prev_values (torch.Tensor): Previous value predictions.\n        value_clip (float): Value clipping threshold.\n        huber_delta (float): Huber loss delta parameter.\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple[torch.Tensor, Dict]: (critic_loss, metrics_dict)\n    \"\"\"\n    loss_mask_ratio = None\n    loss_agg_func = masked_mean\n\n    if (\n        max_episode_steps is not None\n        and loss_mask_sum is not None\n        and loss_mask is not None\n    ):\n        loss_mask_ratio = (loss_mask_sum * 1.0) / max_episode_steps\n        loss_agg_func = masked_mean_ratio\n\n    value_pred_clipped = prev_values + (values - prev_values).clamp(\n        -value_clip, value_clip\n    )  # [bsz, ] | [bsz, chunk-step]\n\n    value_loss_original = huber_loss(\n        returns - values, huber_delta\n    )  # [bsz, ] | [bsz, chunk-step]\n    value_loss_clipped = huber_loss(\n        returns - value_pred_clipped, huber_delta\n    )  # [bsz, ] | [bsz, chunk-step]\n    value_loss = torch.max(value_loss_original, value_loss_clipped)\n    value_loss = loss_agg_func(value_loss, loss_mask, loss_mask_ratio)\n\n    value_clip_indicator = (value_pred_clipped - prev_values).abs() > value_clip\n    value_clip_ratio = value_clip_indicator.float().mean()\n\n    explained_variance_stats = compute_critic_explained_variance_stats(\n        returns=returns,\n        values=values,\n        loss_mask=loss_mask,\n    )\n\n    # Compile metrics for logging\n    metrics_data = {\n        \"critic/value_loss\": value_loss.detach(),\n        \"critic/value_clip_ratio\": value_clip_ratio.detach(),\n    }\n    metrics_data.update(\n        {key: value.detach() for key, value in explained_variance_stats.items()}\n    )\n    return value_loss, metrics_data\n\n\n@register_policy_loss(\"decoupled_actor_critic\")\ndef compute_decoupled_ppo_actor_critic_loss(**kwargs) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, dict]:\n    \"\"\"Compute decoupled PPO actor+critic loss.\"\"\"\n    metrics_data = {}\n    actor_loss, actor_metrics_data = compute_decoupled_ppo_actor_loss(**kwargs)\n    critic_loss, critic_metrics_data = compute_ppo_critic_loss(**kwargs)\n\n    loss = actor_loss + critic_loss\n    metrics_data.update(actor_metrics_data)\n    metrics_data.update(critic_metrics_data)\n    return loss, metrics_data\n\n\n@register_policy_loss(\"actor_critic\")\ndef compute_ppo_actor_critic_loss(**kwargs) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, dict]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute PPO actor loss function.\n\n    Args:\n        logprobs (torch.Tensor): Log probabilities of actions\n        values (torch.Tensor): Current value predictions\n        old_log_prob (torch.Tensor): Previous log probabilities\n        advantages (torch.Tensor): Advantage values\n        returns (torch.Tensor): Return values\n        prev_values (torch.Tensor): Previous value predictions\n Huber loss delta parameter\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple[torch.Tensor, Dict]: Loss and metrics dictionary\n    \"\"\"\n    metrics_data = {}\n    actor_loss, actor_metrics_data = compute_ppo_actor_loss(**kwargs)\n    critic_loss, critic_metrics_data = compute_ppo_critic_loss(**kwargs)\n\n    loss = actor_loss + critic_loss\n    metrics_data.update(actor_metrics_data)\n    metrics_data.update(critic_metrics_data)\n\n    return loss, metrics_data\n\n\n@register_policy_loss(\"opd\")\ndef compute_opd_actor_loss(\n    logprobs: torch.Tensor,\n    advantages: torch.Tensor,\n    loss_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n    loss_agg_func: Optional[Callable[..., torch.Tensor]] = masked_mean,\n    max_episode_steps: Optional[int] = None,\n    loss_mask_sum: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n    **kwargs,\n) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, dict]:\n    \"\"\"Compute the VLA-OPD actor loss with stop-gradient dense rewards.\"\"\"\n    assert logprobs.dtype == torch.float32, (\n        \"logprobs must be float32 to keep numerical stability\"\n    )\n    assert advantages.dtype == torch.float32, (\n        \"advantages must be float32 to keep numerical stability\"\n    )\n    assert advantages.shape == logprobs.shape, (\n        f\"OPD advantages shape {advantages.shape} must match logprobs shape \"\n        f\"{logprobs.shape}.\"\n    )\n    assert loss_mask is not None, \"OPD actor loss requires loss_mask.\"\n    assert loss_mask_sum is not None, \"OPD actor loss requires loss_mask_sum.\"\n\n    if loss_mask.dim() == logprobs.dim() - 1:\n        loss_mask = loss_mask.unsqueeze(-1)\n    if loss_mask_sum.dim() == logprobs.dim() - 1:\n        loss_mask_sum = loss_mask_sum.unsqueeze(-1)\n    if loss_mask.shape != logprobs.shape:\n        assert loss_mask.dim() == logprobs.dim(), (\n            f\"OPD loss_mask rank {loss_mask.dim()} must match logprobs rank \"\n            f\"{logprobs.dim()}.\"\n        )\n        assert loss_mask.shape[:-1] == logprobs.shape[:-1], (\n            f\"OPD loss_mask shape {loss_mask.shape} must match logprobs shape \"\n            f\"{logprobs.shape} except the token dimension.\"\n        )\n        assert loss_mask.shape[-1] == 1, (\n            f\"OPD loss_mask token dimension {loss_mask.shape[-1]} must be 1 \"\n            f\"or match logprobs token dimension {logprobs.shape[-1]}.\"\n        )\n        loss_mask = loss_mask.expand_as(logprobs)\n    if loss_mask_sum.shape != logprobs.shape:\n        assert loss_mask_sum.dim() == logprobs.dim(), (\n            f\"OPD loss_mask_sum rank {loss_mask_sum.dim()} must match \"\n            f\"logprobs rank {logprobs.dim()}.\"\n        )\n        assert loss_mask_sum.shape[:-1] == logprobs.shape[:-1], (\n            f\"OPD loss_mask_sum shape {loss_mask_sum.shape} must match \"\n            f\"logprobs shape {logprobs.shape} except the token dimension.\"\n        )\n        assert loss_mask_sum.shape[-1] == 1, (\n            f\"OPD loss_mask_sum token dimension {loss_mask_sum.shape[-1]} \"\n            f\"must be 1 or match logprobs token dimension {logprobs.shape[-1]}.\"\n        )\n        loss_mask_sum = loss_mask_sum.expand_as(logprobs)\n    assert loss_mask.shape == logprobs.shape, (\n        f\"OPD loss_mask shape {loss_mask.shape} must match logprobs shape \"\n        f\"{logprobs.shape}.\"\n    )\n    assert loss_mask_sum.shape == logprobs.shape, (\n        f\"OPD loss_mask_sum shape {loss_mask_sum.shape} must match \"\n        f\"logprobs shape {logprobs.shape}.\"\n    )\n\n    loss_mask_ratio = None\n    if max_episode_steps is not None:\n        loss_mask_ratio = (loss_mask_sum * 1.0) / max_episode_steps\n        loss_agg_func = masked_mean_ratio\n\n    opd_rewards = advantages.detach()\n    policy_loss = loss_agg_func(-logprobs * opd_rewards, loss_mask, loss_mask_ratio)\n\n    metrics_data = {\n        \"actor/policy_loss\": policy_loss.detach(),\n        \"actor/opd_reward\": masked_mean(opd_rewards, loss_mask).detach(),\n        \"actor/opd_reverse_kl\": masked_mean(-opd_rewards, loss_mask).detach(),\n    }\n    return   \"\"\"\n    Compute the masked mean of absolute difference between rollout and training logprobs.\n\n    Args:\n        m_batch: Dictionary containing 'rollout_logprobs' and 'recomputed_logprobs'.\n  if self.is_weight_offloaded:\n                self.load_param_and_grad(self.device, False)\n\n        rollout_dtype = None\n        if self._cfg.get(\"sync_precision\", None) is not None:\n   has_visual = any(\"visual.\" in k for k in rollout_state_dict.keys())\n        model_bucket_list = self.divide_model_to_bucket(rollout_state_dict, has_visual)\n        del rollout_state_dict\n        send_handles = []\n        buffer = {}\n        for bucket_idx, model_bucket in enumerate(model_bucket_list):\n            for k, v in model_bucket.items():\n                if isinstance(v, DTensor):\n                    v = v.full_tensor()\n                if rollout_dtype is not None:\n                    v = v.to(rollout_dtype)\n                if not self.is_pipeline:\n                    v = reduce_tensor(v)\n                buffer[k] = v\n            if bucket_idx == 0:\n                buffer[\"bucket_length\"] = len(model_bucket_list)\n\n            for send_handle in send_handles:\n                send_handle.wait()\n            send_handles = []\n\n            if not self.is_pipeline:\n                send_handle = self.send(\n                    buffer,\n                    self._rollout_group_name,\n                    self._weight_dst_rank_in_rollout,\n                    async_op=True,\n                )\n                send_handles.append(send_handle)\n            else:\n                for rank in self._weight_dst_rank_in_rollout:\n                    send_handle = self.send(\n                        buffer,\n                        self._rollout_group_name,\n                        rank,\n                        async_op=True,\n                    )\n                    send_handles.append(send_handle)\n            buffer = {}\n\n        for send_handle in send_handles:\n            send_handle.wait()\n\n        if self.enable_offload:\n            assert not self.is_weight_offloaded, (\n                \"weight should be offloaded in sync_model_to_rollout\"\n            )\n            self.offload_param_and_grad()\n\n        clear_memory(sync=False)\n\n    def get_batch(\n        self, channel: Channel\n    ) -> tuple[dict[str, torch.Tensor], RolloutResult]:\n        result: RolloutResult = channel.get()\n\n        batch = result.to_actor_batch(\n            self.cfg.data.max_prompt_length,\n            self.cfg.actor.model.encoder_seq_length,\n            self.tokenizer.eos_token_id,\n        )\n        return batch, result\n\n    def get_dynamic_batch_as_much(\n        self,\n        input_channel: Channel,\n        min_result_len: int,\n        max_result_len: int,\n        cliped_results=[],\n        unfinished_result=None,\n    ):\n        assert not input_channel.is_local\n        rollout_results = cliped_results\n        # get min_result_len\n        while len(rollout_results) < min_result_len:\n            if unfinished_result is not None:\n                rollout_result: RolloutResult = unfinished_result.wait()\n                unfinished_result = None\n            else:\n                rollout_result: RolloutResult = input_channel.get()\n            rollout_results.append(rollout_result)\n\n        # try to get result as much\n        # get result in every 0.1s and do all reduce to get the min result between dp (result_len)\n        # stop at: the min result between dp (result_len) is same as the last min result\n        last_result_len = 0\n        result_len = len(rollout_results)\n        time_until = time.time() + 0.1\n        while last_result_len < result_len:\n            if len(rollout_results) < max_result_len:\n                if unfinished_result is None:\n                    unfinished_result = input_channel.get(async_op=True)\n                else:\n                    time.sleep(0.001)\n                if unfinished_result.done():\n                    rollout_results.append(unfinished_result.wait())\n                    unfinished_result = None\n                if time.time() >= time_until:\n                    last_result_len = result_len\n                    result_len = all_reduce_int(len(rollout_results))\n                    if last_result_len < result_len:\n                        time_until = time.time() + 0.1\n            else:\n                last_result_len = result_len\n                result_len = all_reduce_int(len(rollout_results))\n\n        cliped_results = list(rollout_results[result_len:])\n        rollout_results = rollout_results[:result_len]\n\n        batches = []\n        for rollout_result in rollout_results:\n            batch = rollout_result.to_actor_batch(\n                self.cfg.data.max_prompt_length,\n                self.cfg.actor.model.encoder_seq_length,\n                self.tokenizer.eos_token_id,\n            )\n            batches.append(batch)\n\n        batch = RolloutResult.merge_batches(batches)\n        rollout_result = RolloutResult.merge_result_list(rollout_results)\n        return batch, rollout_result, result_len, cliped_results, unfinished_result\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def _split_to_micro_batch(\n        batch,\n        enable_dynamic_batch_size: bool,\n        *,\n        max_tokens_per_mbs: Optional[int] = None,\n        split_num,\n    ):\n        if enable_dynamic_batch_size:\n            (\n                micro_batches_iter,\n                _,\n                micro_batch_cnt,\n                dbs_indices,\n            ) = split_dynamic_batch_size(\n                batch=batch,\n                cp_world_size=1,\n                vpp_world_size=1,\n                max_tokens_per_mbs=max_tokens_per_mbs,\n                microbatch_group_size_per_vp_stage=1,\n            )\n        else:\n            micro_batch_cnt = split_num\n            micro_batches_iter = get_iterator_k_split(batch, micro_batch_cnt)\n            dbs_indices = None\n        return micro_batches_iter, micro_batch_cnt, dbs_indices\n\n    def _load_weight_and_optimizer(self) -> None:\n        # Acquire the GPUs to ensure that no one is using them before loading models\n        # Otherwise, it may lead to OOM\n        with self.device_lock:\n            if not self.enable_offload:\n                return\n            if self.is_weight_offloaded:\n                self.load_param_and_grad(self.device)\n            if self.is_optimizer_offloaded:\n                self.load_optimizer(self.device)\n\n    def compute_logprobs(self, logits, target):\n        return compute_logprobs_from_logits(\n            logits,\n            target,\n            op_type=self.entropy_op_type,\n        )\n\n    def forward_batch(\n        self, m_batch: dict[str, torch.Tensor], calculate_entropy: bool = False\n    ) -> torch.Tensor:\n        input_ids = m_batch[\"input_ids\"]\n        attention_mask = m_batch[\"attention_mask\"]\n        position_ids = m_batch[\"position_ids\"]\n\n        multi_modal_inputs = {}\n        if \"multi_modal_inputs\" in m_batch.keys():\n            for key in m_batch[\"multi_modal_inputs\"][0].keys():\n                multi_modal_inputs[key] = torch.cat(\n                    [inputs[key] for inputs in m_batch[\"multi_modal_inputs\"]],\n                    dim=0,\n                ).to(Worker.torch_device_type)\n\n        if self.enable_dynamic_batch_size or self.variable_seq_lengths:\n            max_seq_len_pack = self.max_tokens_per_mbs\n            max_seq_len_unpack = self.cfg.actor.model.encoder_seq_length\n            max_prompt_len = self.cfg.data.max_prompt_length\n            max_response_len = max_seq_len_unpack - max_prompt_len\n            idx_starts, idx_ends = prepare_pack_fsdp(m_batch, max_prompt_len)\n\n            input_ids, position_ids, attention_mask = pack_fsdp_input(\n                input_ids,\n                position_ids,\n                idx_starts=idx_starts,\n                idx_ends=idx_ends,\n                max_seq_len_pack=max_seq_len_pack,\n                eos_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id,\n                pad_to_fixed_len=not self.variable_seq_lengths,\n            )\n\n        with self.amp_context:\n            outputs = self.model(\n                input_ids=input_ids,\n                attention_mask=attention_mask,\n                position_ids=position_ids,\n                use_cache=False,\n                **multi_modal_inputs,\n            )\n\n        logits: torch.Tensor = outputs.logits\n\n        logits.div_(self.cfg.algorithm.sampling_params.temperature)\n\n        if self.enable_dynamic_batch_size or self.variable_seq_lengths:\n            logprobs = unpack_fsdp_logprobs(\n                logits,\n                input_ids,\n                idx_starts=idx_starts,\n                idx_ends=idx_ends,\n                max_seq_len_unpack=max_seq_len_unpack,\n                eos_token_id=self.tokenizer.eos_token_id,\n                compute_logprobs_fn=self.compute_logprobs,\n            )\n            logprobs = logprobs[:, -max_response_len:]\n        else:\n            # (bsz, response_length, vocab_size)\n            logits = logits[:, -self.response_len - 1 : -1, :]\n            responses = input_ids[:, -self.response_len :]\n            logprobs = self.compute_logprobs(logits, responses)\n\n        if calculate_entropy:\n            entropy = compute_entropy_from_logits(logits)\n\n            if self.enable_dynamic_batch_size or self.variable_seq_lengths:\n                entropy = unpack_sequences(\n                    entropy, idx_starts, idx_ends, max_seq_len_unpack, pad_val=0\n                )[:, -self.response_len :]\n\n            return logprobs, entropy\n\n        return logprobs\n\n    def inference_step(\n        self,\n        batch: dict[str, torch.Tensor],\n        num_sequences: int,\n        compute_ref_logprobs: bool,\n    ):\n        micro_batches_iter, _, dbs_indices = self._split_to_micro_batch(\n            batch,\n            self.enable_dynamic_batch_size,\n            max_tokens_per_mbs=self.max_tokens_per_mbs,\n            split_num=num_sequences\n            // self.cfg.algorithm.logprob_forward_micro_batch_size,\n        )\n        if self.enable_dynamic_batch_size:\n            indices = sum(dbs_indices, [])\n            revert_indices = torch.tensor(\n                get_reverse_idx(indices),\n                dtype=torch.long,\n            )\n        micro_batches = list(micro_batches_iter)\n\n        recomputed_logprobs, ref_logprobs = None, None\n\n        # Recompute logprobs\n        recomputed_logprobs = torch.cat(\n            [self.forward_batch(batch) for batch in micro_batches]\n        ).cpu()\n\n        if self.enable_dynamic_batch_size:\n            assert len(indices) == recomputed_logprobs.size(0), (\n                f\"Dynamic batch size indices length {len(indices)} does not equal \"\n                f\"output length {recomputed_logprobs.size(0)}\"\n            )\n            recomputed_logprobs = recomputed_logprobs[revert_indices]\n\n        # Ref logprobs\n        if compute_ref_logprobs:\n            assert self.ref_policy_state_dict is not None, (\n                \"Reference policy state dict is None but compute_ref_logprobs is True\"\n            )\n            with cpu_weight_swap(\n                self.model,\n                self.ref_policy_state_dict,\n                self.offload_model_buffer,\n            ):\n                ref_logprobs = torch.cat(\n                    [self.forward_batch(batch) for batch in micro_batches]\n                ).cpu()\n\n                if self.enable_dynamic_batch_size:\n                    assert len(indices) == ref_logprobs.size(0), (\n                        f\"Dynamic batch size indices length {len(indices)} does not equal \"\n                        f\"output length {ref_logprobs.size(0)}\"\n                    )\n                    ref_logprobs = ref_logprobs[revert_indices]\n\n        return recomputed_logprobs, ref_logprobs\n\n    def run_inference(\n        self,\n        input_channel: Channel,\n        output_channel: Channel,\n        compute_ref_logprobs: bool,\n        do_offload=False,\n    ):\n        \"\"\"\n        Compute prev/ref logprobs using the actor Model's forward.\n\n        Args:\n            input_channel: The input channel to read from.\n            output_channel: The output channel to send results to.\n            compute_ref_logprobs: Whether to compute reference logprobs.\n            do_offload: Whether offload weights after inference is done\n        \"\"\"\n        assert not do_offload, (\n            \"do_offload argument of run_inference/run_training is not supported in FSDP for now\"\n        )\n\n        inference_split = self.cfg.actor.get(\"inference_split\", None)\n        if inference_split is None:\n            if not self.is_pipeline:\n                inference_split = 1\n            else:\n                inference_split = self.cfg.algorithm.n_minibatches\n        assert self.total_batch_size_per_dp % inference_split == 0, (\n            f\"FSDPActor: total_batch_size_per_dp[{self.total_batch_size_per_dp}] should be divisible by inference_split[{inference_split}]\"\n        )\n\n        min_result_len = 1\n        max_result_len = (\n            self.cfg.data.rollout_batch_size // self._world_size // inference_split\n        )\n        if not self.is_pipeline:\n            min_result_len = max_result_len\n            coll_rollout_results = []\n        total_result_len = 0\n        total_result_len_per_dp = self.cfg.data.rollout_batch_size // self._world_size\n        cliped_results, unfinished_result = [], None\n        while total_result_len < total_result_len_per_dp:\n            batch, rollout_result, result_len, cliped_results, unfinished_result = (\n                self.get_dynamic_batch_as_much(\n                    input_channel,\n                    min(min_result_len, total_result_len_per_dp - total_result_len),\n                    min(max_result_len, total_result_len_per_dp - total_result_len),\n                    cliped_results,\n                    unfinished_result,\n                )\n            )\n            total_result_len += result_len\n            self.log_debug(\n                f\"[dynamic inference rank-{self._rank}] inference result_len={result_len}, total_result_len={total_result_len}/{total_result_len_per_dp}\"\n            )\n            self._load_weight_and_optimizer()\n            self.model.eval()\n\n            with self.worker_timer():\n                with torch.no_grad():\n                    recomputed_logprobs, ref_logprobs = self.inference_step(\n                        batch, rollout_result.num_sequence, compute_ref_logprobs\n                    )\n\n                rollout_result.recomputed_logprobs = recomputed_logprobs\n\n                # Ref logprobs\n                if compute_ref_logprobs:\n                    rollout_result.ref_logprobs = ref_logprobs\n\n            if self.is_pipeline:\n                # for pipeline mode, send after inference to reduce latency.\n                # should do split to ensure actor won't get too much batches.\n                split_results = RolloutResult.split_results(rollout_result, result_len)\n                for split_result in split_results:\n                    output_channel.put(split_result, async_op=True)\n            else:\n                coll_rollout_results.append(rollout_result)\n\n        if not self.is_pipeline:\n            # for coll mode, merge results to reduce send time.\n            rollout_result = RolloutResult.merge_result_list(coll_rollout_results)\n            split_results = RolloutResult.split_results(\n                rollout_result,\n                min(total_result_len, self.cfg.algorithm.n_minibatches),\n            )\n            for split_result in split_results:\n                output_channel.put(split_result)\n        assert total_result_len == total_result_len_per_dp, (\n            f\"Expected {total_result_len_per_dp} sequences from channel, but got {total_result_len}\"\n        )\n\n    @Worker.timer(\"training_step\")\n    def training_step(\n        self, batch: dict[str, torch.Tensor] | BatchResizingIterator\n    ) -> tuple[dict[str, torch.Tensor], float, list[float]]:\n        if isinstance(batch, dict):\n            global_batch_size = batch[\"input_ids\"].shape[0]\n            assert global_batch_size % self.micro_batch_size == 0, (\n                f\"global batch size {global_batch_size} can not divide micro_batch_size {self.micro_batch_size}\"\n            )\n            micro_batches_iter, micro_batch_cnt, _ = self._split_to_micro_batch(\n                batch,\n                self.enable_dynamic_batch_size,\n                max_tokens_per_mbs=self.max_tokens_per_mbs,\n                split_num=global_batch_size // self.micro_batch_size,\n            )\n            self.gradient_accumulation = micro_batch_cnt\n        else:\n            global_batch_size = self.total_batch_size_per_dp // self.n_mini_batches\n            micro_batch_cnt = global_batch_size // self.micro_batch_size\n            self.gradient_accumulation = micro_batch_cnt\n\n            def iterator_wrapper():\n                for _ in range(micro_batch_cnt):\n                    yield next(batch)\n\n            micro_batches_iter = iterator_wrapper()\n        self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n        mbs_metrics_list = {}\n        for idx, m_batch in enumerate(micro_batches_iter):\n            backward_ctx = self.before_micro_batch(\n                self.model,\n                is_last_micro_batch=(idx + 1) == micro_batch_cnt,\n            )\n            for k, v in m_batch.items():\n                m_batch[k] = (\n                    v.to(Worker.torch_device_type) if isinstance(v, torch.Tensor) else v\n                )\n\n            # batch for forward\n            logprobs, entropy = self.forward_batch(m_batch, True)\n\n            # batch for backward\n            # Prefer recomputed_logprobs (from actor inference), fallback to rollout_logprobs\n            old_logprobs = m_batch.get(\"recomputed_logprobs\")\n            if old_logprobs is None:\n                old_logprobs = m_batch[\"rollout_logprobs\"]\n            advantages = m_batch[\"advantages\"]\n            ref_logprobs = None\n            if \"ref_logprobs\" in m_batch:\n                ref_logprobs = m_batch[\"ref_logprobs\"]\n\n            loss_mask = m_batch[\"response_mask\"][:, -self.response_len :]\n\n            clip_ratio = self.cfg.algorithm.ratio_clip_eps\n            clip_ratio_low = self.cfg.algorithm.get(\"clip_ratio_low\", None)\n            clip_ratio_high = self.cfg.algorithm.get(\"clip_ratio_high\", None)\n            clip_ratio_low = (\n                clip_ratio_low if clip_ratio_low is not None else clip_ratio\n            )\n            clip_ratio_high = (\n                clip_ratio_high if clip_ratio_high is not None else clip_ratio\n            )\n            clip_ratio_c = self.cfg.algorithm.get(\"clip_ratio_c\", 3.0)\n\n            if self.cfg.algorithm.get(\"importance_sampling_fix\", False):\n                if (\n                    \"rollout_logprobs\" not in m_batch\n                    or \"recomputed_logprobs\" not in m_batch\n                ):\n                    raise ValueError(\n                        \"importance_sampling_fix requires both rollout_logprobs and recomputed_logprobs\"\n                    )\n                rollout_logprobs = m_batch[\"rollout_logprobs\"]\n                recomputed_logprobs = m_batch[\"recomputed_logprobs\"]\n                advantages = advantages * torch.clamp(\n                    (recomputed_logprobs - rollout_logprobs).exp(),\n                    max=self.cfg.algorithm.importance_sampling_clip,\n                )\n\n            loss, mbs_metrics_data = policy_loss(\n                task_type=self.task_type,\n                loss_type=self.cfg.algorithm.loss_type,\n                loss_agg_func=self.loss_agg_func,\n                logprobs=logprobs,\n                old_logprobs=old_logprobs,\n                advantages=advantages,\n                clip_ratio_c=clip_ratio_c,\n                clip_ratio_low=clip_ratio_low,\n                clip_ratio_high=clip_ratio_high,\n                loss_mask=loss_mask,\n                clip_log_ratio_min=self.cfg.algorithm.get(\"clip_log_ratio_min\", None),\n                clip_log_ratio_max=self.cfg.algorithm.get(\"clip_log_ratio_max\", None),\n                fast_path_zero_loss_mask=True,\n            )\n\n            entropy_loss = torch.tensor(\n                0.0, device=Worker.torch_platform.current_device()\n            )\n            if self.calculate_entropy:\n                entropy_loss = self.loss_agg_func(entropy, mask=loss_mask)\n                if self.calculate_entropy_loss:\n                    loss = loss - self.cfg.algorithm.entropy_bonus * entropy_loss\n\n            kl_loss = torch.tensor(0.0, device=Worker.torch_platform.current_device())\n            if self.kl_beta > 0 and ref_logprobs is not None:\n                kld = kl_penalty(ref_logprobs, logprobs, self.kl_penalty_type)\n                kl_loss = self.loss_agg_func(kld, loss_mask)\n                loss = loss + kl_loss * self.kl_beta\n\n            # add to log\n            # scale loss for gradient accumulation and backprop\n            final_loss_metric = loss.detach()\n            loss = loss / self.gradient_accumulation\n            with backward_ctx:\n                self.grad_scaler.scale(loss).backward()\n\n            mbs_metrics_data.update(\n                {\n                    \"actor/final_loss\": final_loss_metric,\n                    \"actor/entropy_loss\": entropy_loss.detach(),\n                    \"actor/kl_loss\": kl_loss.detach(),\n                }\n            )\n\n            append_to_dict(mbs_metrics_list, mbs_metrics_data)\n\n        grad_norm, lr_list = self.optimizer_step()\n\n        if self.lr_sched_sync_with_optim:\n            self.lr_scheduler.step()\n\n        # display the degree of mismatch between training and rollout\n        rollout_train_kl = compute_rollout_train_kl(m_batch, loss_mask)\n\n        # aggregate metrics across micro-batches\n        explained_variance_stats = pop_critic_explained_variance_stats(mbs_metrics_list)\n        mean_metric_dict = {\n            key: torch.mean(torch.stack(value))\n            for key, value in mbs_metrics_list.items()\n        }\n        if rollout_train_kl is not None:\n            mean_metric_dict[\"actor/rollout_train_kl\"] = rollout_train_kl\n\n        mean_metric_dict = all_reduce_dict(\n            mean_metric_dict, op=torch.distributed.ReduceOp.AVG\n        )\n        if explained_variance_stats:\n            reduced_stats = all_reduce_dict(\n                explained_variance_stats, op=torch.distributed.ReduceOp.SUM\n            )\n            mean_metric_dict[CRITIC_EXPLAINED_VARIANCE_KEY] = (\n                compute_critic_explained_variance_from_stats(reduced_stats).item()\n            )\n\n        mean_metric_dict[\"actor/grad_norm\"] = float(grad_norm)\n        mean_metric_dict[\"actor/lr\"] = lr_list[0]\n        return mean_metric_dict\n\n    def run_training_pipeline(self, input_channel: Channel) -> tuple[dict, list]:\n        self.model.train()\n        train_batch_iterator = BatchResizingIterator(\n            cfg=self.cfg,\n            get_batch_fn=partial(self.get_batch, input_channel),\n            micro_batch_size=self.micro_batch_size,\n            total_batch_size=self.total_batch_size_per_dp,\n            num_global_batches=self.n_mini_batches,\n            forward_only=False,\n        )\n        train_batch_iterator.register_get_batch_handler(\n            self.compute_advantages_and_returns\n        )\n\n        if self.cfg.algorithm.normalize_advantages:\n\n            def normalize_advantages(batch: dict[str, torch.Tensor]):\n                mask = batch[\"response_mask\"][:, -self.response_len :]\n                batch[\"advantages\"] = masked_normalization(batch[\"advantages\"], mask)\n                return batch\n\n            train_batch_iterator.register_global_batch_handler(normalize_advantages)\n\n        self._load_weight_and_optimizer()\n        training_metrics_list = []\n        with self.worker_timer(\"run_training\"):\n            for _ in range(self.n_mini_batches):\n                mean_metric_dict = self.training_step(batch=train_batch_iterator)\n                training_metrics_list.append(mean_metric_dict)\n            if not self.lr_sched_sync_with_optim:\n                self.lr_scheduler.step()\n\n        # Rollout metrics\n        batch = train_batch_iterator.get_all_batches()\n        rollout_metrics, _, _ = compute_math_rollout_metrics(\n            batch, self.cfg.data.max_prompt_length, self.response_len\n        )\n\n        return rollout_metrics, training_metrics_list\n\n    def _dp_load_balance(self, batch: dict[str, torch.Tensor]):\n        batch_size = batch[\"input_ids\"].shape[0]\n        assert batch_size == self.total_batch_size_per_dp, (\n            f\"DP Load balance is only available when a single batch contains all data, e.g., in collocated mode. But got {batch_size=} and {self.total_batch_size_per_dp=}.\"\n        )\n        batch = RolloutDataBalance.from_rollout_batches(\n            rollout_batches=batch,\n            dp_world_size=torch.distributed.get_world_size(),\n            dp_rank=torch.distributed.get_rank(),\n            dp_group=torch.distributed.group.WORLD,\n            partitioning_tool=get_seqlen_balanced_partitions,\n        )\n        return batch\n\n    @Worker.timer(\"run_training\")\n    def run_training(\n        self, input_channel: Channel, do_offload=False\n    ) -> tuple[dict, list]:\n        # Get all batches for this DP\n        assert not do_offload, (\n            \"do_offload argument of run_inference/run_training is not supported in FSDP for now\"\n        )\n\n        if self.is_pipeline:\n            return self.run_training_pipeline(input_channel)\n\n        batches = []\n        recv_batch_size = 0\n        while recv_batch_size < self.total_batch_size_per_dp:\n            batch, rollout_result = self.get_batch(input_channel)\n            batches.append(batch)\n            recv_batch_size += rollout_result.num_sequence\n        assert recv_batch_size == self.total_batch_size_per_dp, (\n            f\"Expected {self.total_batch_size_per_dp} sequences from channel, but got {recv_batch_size}\"\n        )\n        global_batch = RolloutResult.merge_batches(batches)\n\n        assert (\n            \"recomputed_logprobs\" in global_batch or \"rollout_logprobs\" in global_batch\n        )\n\n        # Compute advantages and returns\n        global_batch = self.compute_advantages_and_returns(global_batch)\n\n        if self.enable_dp_load_balance:\n            global_batch = self._dp_load_balance(global_batch)\n\n        if self.cfg.algorithm.normalize_advantages:\n            mask = global_batch[\"response_mask\"][:, -self.response_len :]\n            global_batch[\"advantages\"] = masked_normalization(\n                global_batch[\"advantages\"], mask\n            )\n\n        # Must be called after batch is retrieved, which is when rollout has stopped\n        # Otherwise, loading model might cause OOM\n        self._load_weight_and_optimizer()\n\n        mini_batches = get_iterator_k_split(\n            global_batch,\n            num_splits=self.cfg.algorithm.n_minibatches,\n            shuffle=self.cfg.algorithm.get(\"shuffle_rollout\", True),\n            shuffle_seed=self.cfg.actor.seed,\n        )\n\n        self.model.train()\n        assert (\n            self.cfg.actor.global_batch_size\n            % (self.cfg.actor.micro_batch_size * self._world_size)\n            == 0\n        )\n\n        training_metrics_list = []\n        # Global batch iterations\n        with self.worker_timer():\n            for mini_batch in mini_batches:\n                mean_metric_dict = self.training_step(batch=mini_batch)\n                training_metrics_list.append(mean_metric_dict)\n            if not self.lr_sched_sync_with_optim:\n                self.lr_scheduler.step()\n\n        # Rollout metrics\n        rollout_metrics, _, _ = compute_math_rollout_metrics(\n            global_batch, self.cfg.data.max_prompt_length, self.response_len\n        )\n\n        return rollout_metrics, training_metrics_list\n\n    # Advantages and returns\n    @Worker.timer(\"compute_advantages_and_returns\")\n    def compute_advantages_and_returns(self, batch: dict[str, torch.Tensor]):\n        \"\"\"Compute the advantages and returns.\n\n        Args:\n            batch (Dict[str, torch.Tensor]): The rollout batch.\n        \"\"\"\n        with self.worker_timer():\n            if batch.get(\"advantages\", None) is None:\n                mask = batch[\"response_mask\"][:, -self.response_len :]\n                logprob = batch.get(\"recomputed_logprobs\")\n                if logprob is None:\n                    logprob = batch.get(\"rollout_logprobs\")\n                logprob = logprob.to(Worker.torch_device_type)\n\n                advantages, _ = calculate_adv_and_returns(\n                    task_type=self.task_type,\n                    adv_type=self.cfg.algorithm.adv_type,\n                    rewards=batch[\"rewards\"].to(Worker.torch_device_type),\n                    loss_mask=mask.to(Worker.torch_device_type),\n                    group_size=self.cfg.algorithm.group_size,\n                    kl_beta=self.reinpp_kl_beta,\n                    kl_penalty_type=self.kl_penalty_type,\n                    logprob=logprob,\n                    ref_logprob=batch[\"ref_logprobs\"].to(Worker.torch_device_type)\n                    if \"ref_logprobs\" in batch\n                    else None,\n                    use_reinpp_baseline=self.cfg.algorithm.get(\n                        \"use_reinpp_baseline\", False\n                    ),\n                )\n                batch[\"advantages\"] = advantages\n        return batch\n"}

    self, actions: Union[torch.Tensor, np.ndarray, dict] = None, auto_reset=True\n    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, dict]:\n        if actions is None:\n from being freed prematurely and allocated again by pytorch caching allocator.\n    The caller should synchronize before clearing these tensors.\n\n    Args:\n        tensors (Iterable[torch.Tensor]): seq_mean_token_sum(values: torch.Tensor, mask: torch.Tensor, dim: int = -1):\n    seq_losses = torch.sum(values * mask, dim=-1)  # token-sum\n    loss = torch.mean(seq_losses)  # seq-mean\n    return   action_dim: int = 7,\n    batch_size: int = 1,\n) -> Optional[torch.Tensor]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Reshape entropy based on the entropy type.If entropy is None, return None.\n    If entropy_type is \"action_level\", reshape entropy to [batch_size, seq_len] by summing over action_dim.\n    If entropy_type is \"chunk_level\", reshape entropy to [batch_size, seq_len]\n\n    Args:\n        entropy(Optional[torch.Tensor]): [B, seq_len * action_dim] or [B, seq_len] or None\n        entropy_type(str): \"action_level\" or \"chunk_level\"\n        action_dim(int): action dimension, default logprobs_from_logits_flash_attn(\n    logits: torch.Tensor, labels: torch.Tensor, inplace_backward: bool = True\n) -> torch.Tensor:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute logprobs by logits using flash-attn's \"\"\"\n    from liger_kernel.transformers.cross_entropy import LigerCrossEntropyLoss\n\n    loss_func = LigerCrossEntropyLoss(reduction=\"none\")\n    logprobs = -loss_func(logits, labels)\n torch.Tensor:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute logprobs by logits.\n\n    Args:\n        logits(torch.Tensor): [B, seq-len, vocab-size]\n        target(torch.Tensor): [B, seq-len]\n        op_type(str): the type of logprobs computation method, options are \"torch\", \"flash_attn\", \"liger_kernel\"\n            default is \"torch\".\n            flash_attn calc in fp32, torch and liger_kernel calc in logits.dtype.\n\n    Returns:\n        logprobs(torch.Tensor): [B, seq-len]\n    \"\"\"\n    batch_dim = logits.shape[:-1]\n    last_dim = logits.shape[-1]\n    logits = logits.reshape(-1, last_dim)\n    labels = target.reshape(-1)\n\n    assert op_type in [\"torch\", \"flash_attn\", \"liger_kernel\"], (\n        f\"Unsupported op_type: {op_type} for logprobs computation. Supported types are 'torch', 'flash_attn', 'liger_kernel'.\"\n    )\n    if op_type == \"liger_kernel\":\n        # liger_kernel will use input dtype to compute logprobs.\n        logprobs = logprobs_from_logits_liger_kernel(logits, labels)\n    elif op_type == \"flash_attn\":\n        # flash_attn will use fp32 to compute logprobs.\n        logprobs = logprobs_from_logits_flash_attn(logits, labels)\n    elif op_type == \"torch\":\n        # torch will use input dtype to compute logprobs.\n        logprobs = -F.cross_entropy(logits, labels, reduction=\"none\")\n\n    # reshape back to [B, seq-len]\n    logprobs = logprobs.view(*batch_dim).float()\n    return logprobs\n\n\ndef compute_entropy_from_logits(logits: torch.Tensor, dim: int = -1) -> torch.Tensor:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute entropy by logits,formula: H(X) = - sum(p(x) * log(p(x)))\n    In case logits are too small to cause numerical instability(like downflow to zero after softmax),\n    we use log_softmax to compute(it will automatically stabilize the computation) logp.\n\n    Args:\n        - logits(torch.Tensor): [B,seq-len,vocab-size]\n        - dim(int): the dimension to compute entropy\n    Returns:\n        - entropy(torch.Tensor): [B, seq-len]\n    \"\"\"\n    logp = F.log_softmax(logits, dim=dim)\n    p = logp.exp()\n    # if some p are zero, p*logp will be nan, we set those terms to zero\n    entropy_term = torch.where(p > 0, p * logp, 0.0)\n    entropy = -entropy_term.sum(dim=dim)\n    return entropy\n\n\nclass DualOutput:\n    def __init__(self, file, terminal):\n        self.file = file\n        self.terminal = terminal\n\n    def write(self, message):\n        self.terminal.write(message)\n        self.file.write(message)\n        self.flush()  # Flush immediately to ensure the data is written.\n\n    def flush(self):\n        self.terminal.flush()\n        self.file.flush()\n\n    def fileno(self):\n        # Return the terminal's fileno to maintain expected behavior\n        return self.terminal.fileno()\n\n    def isatty(self):\n        return self.terminal.isatty()\n\n    def close(self):\n        self.flush()\n        self.file.close()\n\n    def readable(self):\n        return False\n\n    def writable(self):\n        return True\n\n    def seekable(self):\n        return False\n\n\ndef output_redirector(func):\n    @wraps(func)\n    def wrapper(cfg, *args, **kwargs):\n        log_path = os.path.join(\n            cfg.runner.output_dir, cfg.runner.experiment_name, \"log\", \"main.log\"\n        )\n        os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(log_path), exist_ok=True)\n\n        f = open(log_path, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\", buffering=1)\n\n        def close():\n DualOutput(f, sys.stderr)\n\n        old_stdout = sys.stdout\n        old_stderr = sys.stderr\n        try:\n            sys.stdout = dual_out\n            sys.stderr = dual_err\n            return func(cfg, *args, **kwargs)\n\n        except Exception as e:\n            import traceback\n\n            error_msg = f\"\\nException occurred: {e}\\n{traceback.format_exc()}\\n\"\n            dual_err.write(error_msg)\n            dual_err.flush()\n            f.flush()\n            raise\n\n        finally:\n            sys.stdout = old_stdout\n            sys.stderr = old_stderr\n\n load_state_dict/set_optimizer_state_dict.\n    This function does not modify parameter values (by temporarily setting lr to zero + using zero gradients\n    to achieve this).\n    Suitable for mainstream optimizers such as Adam/AdamW/SGD/RMSprop/Adagrad/Adamax/Adadelta (including fused/foreach variants).\n    Not suitable for LBFGS (requires closure and multiple forward/backward passes);\n    if using LBFGS, please manually initialize or switch to another optimizer and then switch back.\n    \"\"\"\n    if isinstance(optimizer, torch.optim.LBFGS):\n        raise RuntimeError(\"fake_optimizer_step does not support LBFGS\")\n\n    def zero_grad_like(p):\n        if getattr(p, \"is_meta\", False) or (\n            hasattr(p, \"device\") and p.device.type == \"meta\"\n        ):\n            return None  # skip meta\n        try:\n            if p.layout is torch.strided and not isinstance(p, DTensor):\n                return torch.zeros_like(p, memory_format=torch.preserve_format)\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n        return p.detach().new_zeros(p.shape)\n\n    # backup every param group's lr\n    saved_lrs = []\n    for g in optimizer.param_groups:\n        saved_lrs.append(g.get(\"lr\", None))\n        g[\"lr\"] = 0.0\n\n    # backup every param's grad, and fill zero grad (ensure every param will init state during step())\n    saved_grads = {}\n    all_params = []\n    for g in optimizer.param_groups:\n        for p in g.get(\"params\", []):\n            if p is None:\n                continue\n            all_params.append(p)\n            saved_grads[p] = p.grad  # may be None, save as is\n            if p.grad is None:\n                p.grad = zero_grad_like(p)\n\n    # step to create optimizer.state entries\n    # use torch.no_grad to avoid any unexpected side effects from custom optimizers\n    with torch.no_grad():\n        optimizer.step()\n\n    # restore every param group's lr\n    for g, lr in zip(optimizer.param_groups, saved_lrs):\n        if lr is not None:\n            g[\"lr\"] = lr\n\n    for p in all_params:\n        p.grad = saved_grads[p]\n\n    # The empty step above advances each Adam param's step counter to 1, which biases\n    # the first real update through bias correction and diverges from a freshly-built\n    # optimizer. Reset the step counter to 0 so this state initialization is a true\n    # no-op for training dynamics, while preserving the already-zeroed exp_avg/exp_avg_sq\n    # entries so load_state_dict still finds initialized state.\n    for p in all_params:\n        st = optimizer.state.get(p, {})\n        step = st.get(\"step\", None)\n        if step is None:\n            continue\n        if torch.is_tensor(step):\n            step.zero_()\n        else:\n            st[\"step\"] = 0\n\n\ndef get_rng_state() -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Get the current RNG state for both CPU and CUDA (if   packed_batch: dict[str, Any] = {}\n    for key, value in batch.items():\n        packed_key = (\n            key if not prefix else f\"{prefix}{PIPELINE_BATCH_KEY_SEPARATOR}{key}\"\n        )\n merge_rollout_epochs(batch: dict[str, Any], rollout_epoch: int) -> dict[str, Any]:\n    ret_dict: dict[str, Any] = {}\n    for key, value in batch.items():\n        if isinstance(value, torch.Tensor):\n            # here to merge the batch for embodied data\n            # which is originally in shape [rollout_epoch, B, ...] to [rollout_epoch*B, ...]\n            new_value = value.reshape(rollout_epoch, -1, *value.shape[1:]).transpose(\n                0, 1\n            )\n            new_value = new_value.reshape(new_value.shape[0], -1, *new_value.shape[3:])\n            ret_dict[key] = new_value\n        elif isinstance(value, dict):\n            ret_dict[key] = merge_rollout_epochs(value, rollout_epoch)\n        else:\n            raise ValueError(\n                f\"Unsupported value type in batch: {type(value)} for key: {key}\"\n            )\n    return ret_dict\n\n\ndef preprocess_embodied_batch(\n    batch: dict[str, Any],\n    *,\n    rollout_epoch: int,\n    auto_reset: bool,\n    ignore_terminations: bool,\n    reward_type: str,\n    filter_rewards: bool,\n    group_size: int,\n    rewards_lower_bound: float | None = None,\n    rewards_upper_bound: float | None = None,\n) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:\n    batch = merge_rollout_epochs(batch, rollout_epoch)\n\n    if not auto_reset and not ignore_terminations:\n        dones = batch[\"dones\"]\n        loss_mask, loss_mask_sum = compute_loss_mask(dones)\n\n        if reward_type == \"chunk_level\":\n            loss_mask = loss_mask.any(dim=-1, keepdim=True)\n            loss_mask_sum = loss_mask_sum[..., -1:]\n\n        batch[\"loss_mask\"] = loss_mask\n        batch[\"loss_mask_sum\"] = loss_mask_sum\n\n    if filter_rewards:\n        rewards = batch[\"rewards\"]\n        if batch.get(\"loss_mask\", None) is not None:\n            rewards = rewards * batch[\"loss_mask\"]\n        n_chunk_step, batch_size, _ = rewards.shape\n\n        assert batch_size % group_size == 0, (\n            f\"batch {batch_size} not divisible by group_size {group_size}\"\n        )\n        n_prompts = batch_size // group_size\n\n        rewards = rewards.transpose(0, 1).reshape(rewards.shape[1], -1)\n        reward_matrix = rewards.reshape(n_prompts, group_size, rewards.shape[-1])\n        reward_matrix = reward_matrix.sum(dim=-1)\n        mean_reward_in_group = reward_matrix.mean(dim=1)\n\n        reward_filter_mask = (mean_reward_in_group >= rewards_lower_bound) & (\n            mean_reward_in_group <= rewards_upper_bound\n        )\n        reward_filter_mask = reward_filter_mask.repeat_interleave(group_size)\n        reward_filter_mask = (\n            reward_filter_mask.unsqueeze(0).expand(n_chunk_step, -1).unsqueeze(-1)\n        )\n\n        if batch.get(\"loss_mask\", None) is not None:\n            batch[\"loss_mask\"] = reward_filter_mask & batch[\"loss_mask\"]\n        else:\n            batch[\"loss_mask\"] = reward_filter_mask\n\n    return batch\n"}

float(scheduler.num_train_timesteps)\n    normalized = timesteps.float() / num_train_timesteps\n    next_normalized = normalized + deltas_fp32\n    if not bool((normalized > 0).all()) or not < normalized).all()\n    ):\n        raise ValueError(\"Invalid next points in Fast-WAM action schedule\")\n\n    effective_shift = (\n        float(scheduler.shift) if sigma_shift is None else float(sigma_shift)\n    )\n    return ActionSchedule(\n        timesteps=timesteps,\n        deltas=deltas,\n        normalized_timesteps=normalized,\n        next_normalized_timesteps=next_normalized.clamp_min(0.0),\n        dts=-deltas_fp32,\n        configured_shift=None if sigma_shift is None else float(sigma_shift),\n        effective_shift=effective_shift,\n    )\n\n\ndef prepare_initial_action_latents(\n    *,\n    batch_size: int,\n    action_horizon: int,\n    action_dim: int,\n    device: torch.device | str,\n    dtype: torch.dtype,\n    rand_device: torch.device | str = \"cpu\",\n    seed: int | None = None,\n    broadcast_singleton: bool = False,\n) -> torch.Tensor:\n    \"\"\"Create official-style action noise.\n\n    With ``broadcast_singleton=True`` this is the vectorized equivalent of calling\n    upstream B=1 ``infer_action(seed=seed)`` independently for every environment.\n    Training never uses this option.\n    \"\"\"\n\n    if batch_size <= 0 or action_horizon <= 0 or action_dim <= 0:\n        raise ValueError(\"batch_size, action_horizon and action_dim must be positive\")\n    if broadcast_singleton and seed is None:\n        raise ValueError(\"A fixed seed is required for singleton-broadcast evaluation\")\n\n    rand_device = torch.device(rand_device)\n    generator = (\n        None\n        if seed is None\n        else torch.Generator(device=rand_device).manual_seed(int(seed))\n    )\n    draw_batch = 1 if broadcast_singleton else batch_size\n    latents = torch.randn(\n        (draw_batch, action_horizon, action_dim),\n        generator=generator,\n        device=rand_device,\n        dtype=torch.float32,\n    ).to(device=device, dtype=dtype)\n    if broadcast_singleton:\n        latents = latents.expand(batch_size, -1, -1).clone()\n    return latents\n\n\ndef prepare_rollout_randomness(\n    *,\n    batch_size: int,\n    action_horizon: int,\n    action_dim: int,\n    num_inference_steps: int,\n    device: torch.device | str,\n    rand_device: torch.device | str = \"cpu\",\n    generator: torch.Generator | None = None,\n) -> RolloutRandomness:\n    \"\"\"Generate independent trajectory noise and one OpenPI-style shared index.\"\"\"\n\n    if batch_size <= 0 or action_horizon <= 0 or action_dim <= 0:\n        raise ValueError(\"batch_size, action_horizon and action_dim must be positive\")\n    if num_inference_steps <= 0:\n        raise ValueError(\"num_inference_steps must be positive\")\n    rand_device = torch.device(rand_device)\n    if generator is not None and torch.device(generator.device) != rand_device:\n        raise ValueError(\"generator device must match rand_device\")\n    initial_latents = torch.randn(\n        (batch_size, action_horizon, action_dim),\n        generator=generator,\n        device=rand_device,\n        dtype=torch.float32,\n    )\n    sde_epsilon = torch.randn(\n        (batch_size, action_horizon, action_dim),\n        generator=generator,\n        device=rand_device,\n        dtype=torch.float32,\n    )\n    shared_k = torch.randint(\n        0,\n        num_inference_steps,\n        (1,),\n        generator=generator,\n        device=rand_device,\n        dtype=torch.long,\n    )\n    denoise_inds = shared_k.expand(batch_size).clone()\n    return )\n\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef encode_first_frame_latents(\n    model,\n    input_image: torch.Tensor,\n    *,\n    tiled: bool = False,\n) -> torch.Tensor:\n    \"\"\"Encode a real batch of one-frame videos with the current tensor VAE.\"\"\"\n\n    if input_image.ndim != 4 or input_image.shape[1] != 3:\n        raise ValueError(\n            f\"input_image must be [B,3,H,W], got input_image.shape[0]:\n        raise ValueError(\"Official Fast-WAM VAE wrapper returned an invalid batch\")\n    return latents\n\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef build_action_conditioning(\n    model,\n    tiled=tiled,\n    )\n    timestep_video = torch.zeros(\n        (batch_size,),\n        dtype=first_frame_latents.dtype,\n        device=device,\n    )\n    (\n        video_tokens,\n        _t_video,\n        video_t_mod,\n        video_context,\n        video_context_mask,\n        video_freqs,\n        _f_video,\n        _h_video,\n        _w_video,\n        tokens_per_frame,\n    ) = ActionConditioning,\n) -> torch.Tensor:\n    \"\"\"Grad-enabled twin of upstream ``_predict_action_noise_with_cache``.\"\"\"\n\n    batch_size = x.shape[0]\n    device = _model_device(model)\n _t_action,\n        action_t_mod,\n        action_context,\n        action_context_mask,\n        action_freqs,\n    ) = model.action_expert.prepare(\n        action_tokens=x.to(device=device,
