self.prev_model_actions.shape[1]:\n            return None\n        return self.prev_model_actions[:, executed_horizon:, :]\n\n\ndef build_rtc_target_and_mask(\n    prev_remaining: torch.Tensor | None,\n    horizon: int,\n    action_dim: int,\n    delay_steps: int,\n    device: torch.device,\n    dtype: torch.dtype,\n) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:\n    \"\"\"Build overlap target and soft mask in model action space.\"\"\"\n    batch_size = 1 if prev_remaining is None else prev_remaining.shape[0]\n    target = torch.zeros((batch_size, horizon, action_dim), device=device, dtype=dtype)\n    mask = torch.zeros((batch_size, horizon, 1), device=device, dtype=dtype)\n\n    if prev_remaining is None or prev_remaining.numel() == 0:\n        return target, mask\n\n    overlap = min(prev_remaining.shape[1], horizon)\n    if overlap <= 0:\n        return target, mask\n\n    target[:, :overlap] = prev_remaining[:, :overlap].to(device=device, dtype=dtype)\n    hard_end = min(max(int(delay_steps), 0), overlap)\n    if hard_end > 0:\n        # Actions that are likely to be executed before the new chunk arrives\n        # are treated as hard constraints.\n        mask[:, :hard_end, 0] = 1.0\n    if hard_end < overlap:\n        # Later overlap positions are softly guided so the new plan can still\n        # adapt to the latest camera observation.\n        i = torch.arange(hard_end, overlap, device=device, dtype=dtype)\n        denom = max(float(overlap - hard_end + 1), 1.0)\n        c_i = (overlap - i) / denom\n        soft = c_i * (torch.expm1(c_i) / (math.e - 1.0))\n        mask[:, hard_end:overlap, 0] = soft\n    return target, mask\n\n\n@torch.no_grad()\ndef sample_actions_with_rtc_guidance(\n    model,\n    observation,\n    rtc_context: RTCGuidanceContext,\n    noise: torch.Tensor | None = None,\n    mode: str = \"eval\",\n    compute_values: bool = True,\n) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:\n    \"\"\"Real-world RTC sampler for OpenPI.\"\"\"\n\n    del mode\n\n    bsize = observation.state.shape[0]\n    device = observation.state.device\n    num_steps = model.config.num_steps\n\n    if noise is None:\n        noise = model.sample_noise(\n            (bsize, model.config.action_horizon, model.config.action_dim), device\n        )\n\n    images, img_masks, lang_tokens, lang_masks, state = model._preprocess_observation(\n        observation, train=False\n    )\n    prefix_output, prefix_pad_masks, past_key_values = model._build_prefix_cache(\n        images, img_masks, lang_tokens, lang_masks\n    )\n\n    prev_remaining = (\n        rtc_context.get_prev_remaining() if rtc_context is not None else None\n    )\n    target, mask = build_rtc_target_and_mask(\n        prev_remaining=prev_remaining,\n        horizon=model.config.action_horizon,\n        action_dim=model.config.action_dim,\n        delay_steps=0 if rtc_context is None else rtc_context.delay_steps,\n        device=device,\n        dtype=noise.dtype,\n    )\n\n    x_t = noise\n    chains = [x_t]\n    log_probs = []\n    values = []\n    denoise_inds = torch.full(\n        (bsize, num_steps),\n        -1,\n        device=device,\n        dtype=torch.long,\n    )\n\n    if model.use_vlm_value:\n        values_vlm = model.get_value_from_vlm(prefix_output)\n\n    paper_tau = torch.tensor(0.0, device=device, dtype=noise.dtype)\n    dt = torch.tensor(1.0 / num_steps, device=device, dtype=noise.dtype)\n\n    for idx in range(num_steps):\n        x_t_mean, x_t_std, value_t, _ = model.sample_mean_var_val(\n            x_t=x_t,\n            idx=idx,\n            state=state,\n            prefix_pad_masks=prefix_pad_masks,\n            past_key_values=past_key_values,\n            sample_method=\"flow_ode\",\n            denoise_steps=num_steps,\n            compute_values=compute_values,\n        )\n\n        if prev_remaining is not None and prev_remaining.numel() > 0:\n            # Apply the RTC correction in denoising space before sampling the\n            # next iterate. The clip keeps the guidance bounded near tau=0.\n            guidance_term = (target - x_t_mean) * mask\n            guidance_scale = torch.clamp(\n                (1.0 - paper_tau) / torch.clamp(paper_tau + 1e-4, min=1e-4),\n                max=float(model.config.rtc_guidance_clip),\n            )\n            x_t_mean = x_t_mean + guidance_scale * guidance_term\n\n        noise_step = model.sample_noise(x_t.shape, device)\n        x_t = x_t_mean + noise_step * x_t_std\n        chains.append(x_t)\n        log_probs.append(model.get_logprob_norm(x_t, x_t_mean, x_t_std))\n        if value_t is not None:\n            values.append(value_t)\n        paper_tau = paper_tau + dt\n\n    x_0 = x_t\n    chains = torch.stack(chains, dim=1)\n\n    if log_probs:\n        log_probs = torch.stack(log_probs, dim=1)[\n            :, :, : model.config.action_chunk, : model.config.action_env_dim\n        ].mean(dim=1)\n    else:\n        log_probs = torch.zeros(\n            (bsize, model.config.action_chunk, model.config.action_env_dim),\n            device=device,\n            dtype=noise.dtype,\n        )\n\n    if model.use_vlm_value:\n        values_out = values_vlm[:, None]\n    elif values:\n        values_out = torch.stack(values, dim=1).mean(dim=-1, keepdim=True)\n    else:\n        values_out = torch.zeros((bsize, 1), device=device, dtype=noise.dtype)\n\n    return {\n        \"actions\": x_0,\n        \"chains\": chains,\n        \"prev_logprobs\": log_probs,\n        \"prev_values\": values_out,\n        \"denoise_inds\": denoise_inds,\n    }\n"}

\"vla_loss\": vla_loss,\n            \"rlt_loss\": rlt_loss,\n        }\n\n    def _sft_forward_with_rlt_prefix(self, observation, actions):\n        images, img_masks, lang_tokens, lang_masks, state = (\n            self._preprocess_observation(observation, train=True)\n        )\n\n        noise = self.sample_noise(actions.shape, actions.device)\n        time = self.sample_time(actions.shape[0], actions.device)\n\n        time_expanded = time[:, None, None]\n        x_t = time_expanded * noise + (1 - time_expanded) * actions\n        u_t = noise - actions\n\n        prefix_embs, prefix_pad_masks, prefix_att_masks = self.embed_prefix(\n            images, img_masks, lang_tokens, lang_masks\n        )\n        suffix_embs, suffix_pad_masks, suffix_att_masks, adarms_cond = (\n            self.embed_suffix(state, x_t, time)\n        )\n        backbone_dtype = self.paligemma_with_expert.paligemma.language_model.layers[\n            0\n        ].self_attn.q_proj.weight.dtype\n        if prefix_embs.dtype != backbone_dtype:\n            prefix_embs = prefix_embs.to(dtype=backbone_dtype)\n        if suffix_embs.dtype != backbone_dtype:\n            suffix_embs = suffix_embs.to(dtype=backbone_dtype)\n\n        pad_masks = torch.cat([prefix_pad_masks, suffix_pad_masks], dim=1)\n        att_masks = torch.cat([prefix_att_masks, suffix_att_masks], dim=1)\n        att_2d_masks = make_att_2d_masks(pad_masks, att_masks)\n        position_ids = torch.cumsum(pad_masks, dim=1) - 1\n        att_2d_masks_4d = self._prepare_attention_masks_4d(att_2d_masks)\n\n        def forward_func(\n            prefix_embs, suffix_embs, att_2d_masks_4d, position_ids, adarms_cond\n        ):\n            (prefix_output, suffix_out), _ = self.paligemma_with_expert.forward(\n                attention_mask=att_2d_masks_4d,\n                position_ids=position_ids,\n                past_key_values=None,\n                inputs_embeds=[prefix_embs, suffix_embs],\n                use_cache=False,\n                adarms_cond=[None, adarms_cond],\n            )\n            return prefix_output, suffix_out\n\n        prefix_output, suffix_out = self._apply_checkpoint(\n            forward_func,\n            prefix_embs,\n            suffix_embs,\n            att_2d_masks_4d,\n            position_ids,\n            adarms_cond,\n        )\n\n        suffix_out = suffix_out[:, -self.config.action_horizon :]\n        suffix_out = suffix_out.to(dtype=torch.float32)\n\n        def action_out_proj_func(suffix_out):\n            return self.action_out_proj(suffix_out)\n\n        v_t = self._apply_checkpoint(action_out_proj_func, suffix_out)\n        loss = F.mse_loss(u_t, v_t, reduction=\"none\")\n\n        prefix_output, prefix_pad_masks = self._select_rlt_prefix_embeddings(\n            prefix_output.detach(), prefix_pad_masks, lang_tokens\n        )\n        return loss, prefix_output, prefix_pad_masks\n\n    def _build_rlt_prefix_cache(self, observation, *, train: bool):\n        images, img_masks, lang_tokens, lang_masks, state = (\n            self._preprocess_observation(observation, train=train)\n        )\n        device = next(self.parameters()).device\n        images = [img.to(device) for img in images]\n        img_masks = [img_mask.to(device) for img_mask in img_masks]\n        if lang_tokens is not None:\n            lang_tokens = lang_tokens.to(device)\n        if lang_masks is not None:\n            lang_masks = lang_masks.to(device)\n        state = state.to(device)\n\n        prefix_output, prefix_pad_masks, past_key_values = self._build_prefix_cache(\n            images, img_masks, lang_tokens, lang_masks\n        )\n        return prefix_output, prefix_pad_masks, past_key_values, lang_tokens, state\n\n    def _select_rlt_prefix_embeddings(\n        self, prefix_output, prefix_pad_masks, lang_tokens\n    ):\n        if self.config.rlt_image_only and lang_tokens is not None:\n            num_image_tokens = prefix_output.shape[1] - lang_tokens.shape[1]\n            prefix_output = prefix_output[:, :num_image_tokens]\n            prefix_pad_masks = prefix_pad_masks[:, :num_image_tokens]\n        return prefix_output, prefix_pad_masks\n\n    def _extract_rlt_prefix_embeddings(self, observation, *, train: bool):\n        with torch.no_grad():\n            prefix_output, prefix_pad_masks, _, lang_tokens, _ = (\n                self._build_rlt_prefix_cache(observation, train=train)\n            )\n\n        return self._select_rlt_prefix_embeddings(\n            prefix_output, prefix_pad_masks, lang_tokens\n        )\n\n    def _select_configured_state(self, states):\n        indices = self.config.state_indices\n        if not indices:\n            return states\n        indices = list(indices)\n\n        if hasattr(states, \"shape\"):\n            state_dim = states.shape[-1]\n        else:\n            state_dim = np.asarray(states).shape[-1]\n        if state_dim == len(indices):\n            return states\n        if state_dim <= max(indices):\n            raise ValueError(\n                f\"Cannot select state_indices={indices} from state dim {state_dim}.\"\n            )\n\n        if torch.is_tensor(states):\n            index_tensor = torch.as_tensor(indices, device=states.device)\n            return states.index_select(-1, index_tensor)\n        return np.asarray(states)[..., indices]\n\n    @torch.no_grad()\n    def extract_rlt_obs(\n        self,\n        env_obs: dict[str, Any],\n    ) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:\n        if not self.config.use_rlt or not hasattr(self, \"rlt_module\"):\n            raise ValueError(\"extract_rlt_obs requires )\n        rlt_mask = rlt_prefix_mask if self.config.rlt_use_mask else None\n        z_rl = self.rlt_module.encode_flat(rlt_prefix_output, rlt_mask).to(\n            dtype=torch.float32\n        )\n\n        outputs = self._sample_actions_with_prefix_cache(\n            state,\n            prefix_output,\n            prefix_pad_masks,\n            past_key_values,\n            mode=\"eval\",\n            compute_values=False,\n        )\n        ref_chunk = self.output_transform(\n            {\"actions\": outputs[\"actions\"], \"state\": observation.state}\n        )[\"actions\"]\n        raw_proprio = self._select_configured_state(env_obs[\"states\"])\n        if (\n            isinstance(self.config.config_name, str)\n            and \"maniskill\" in self.config.config_name.lower()\n        ):\n            state_dim = (\n                raw_proprio.shape[-1]\n                if hasattr(raw_proprio, \"shape\")\n                else np.asarray(raw_proprio).shape[-1]\n            )\n dtype=torch.float32),\n        }\n\n    def prepare_dagger_sft_batch(self, batch):\n        \"\"\"Prepare replay-buffer samples for DAgger SFT updates.\"\"\"\n        device = prepare_lerobot_sft_batch(self, batch):\n        \"\"\"Prepare replay-buffer samples for DAgger SFT updates.\"\"\"\n        device = next(self.parameters()).device\n        obs_dict = {}\n        raw_obs_keys = [\n            k\n            for k in batch.keys()\n            if k\n            in [\n                \"image\",\n                \"wrist_image\",\n **kwargs,\n    ) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        # get kwargs\n        compute_values = kwargs.get(\"compute_values\", False)\n        chains = forward_inputs[\"chains\"]\n        denoise_inds = forward_inputs[\"denoise_inds\"]\n        # input transform\n        observation = self.input_transform(forward_inputs, transpose=False)\n        observation = _model.Observation.from_dict(observation)\n        images, img_masks, lang_tokens, lang_masks, state = (\n            self._preprocess_observation(observation, train=False)\n        )\n        # transfer to device\n        device = chains.device\n        images = [img.to(device) for img in images]\n        img_masks = [img_mask.to(device) for img_mask in img_masks]\n        state = state.to(device)\n        # get log prob\n        log_probs, value_t, entropy = self.get_log_prob_value(\n            images,\n            img_masks,\n            lang_tokens,\n            lang_masks,\n            state,\n            chains,\n            denoise_inds,\n            compute_values,\n        )\n        log_probs = log_probs[\n            :, :, : self.config.action_chunk, : self.config.action_env_dim\n        ]\n        entropy = entropy[\n            :, :, : self.config.action_chunk, : self.config.action_env_dim\n        ]\n        # post process\n        log_probs = log_probs.mean(dim=1)\n        entropy = entropy.mean(dim=[1, 2, 3], keepdim=False)[\n            :, None\n        ]  # [:,None] to align with loss-mask shape\n        value_t = value_t.mean(dim=-1, keepdim=False)\n        return {\n            \"logprobs\": log_probs,\n            \"values\": value_t,\n            \"entropy\": entropy,\n        }\n\n    def nft_forward(\n        self,\n        forward_inputs: dict[str, torch.Tensor],\n        **kwargs,\n    ) -> dict[str, Any]:\n        \"\"\"Compute velocity v_theta at explicit (x_t, timesteps) for NFT loss.\"\"\"\n        # obs process\n        observation = self.input_transform(forward_inputs, transpose=False)\n        observation = _model.Observation.from_dict(observation)\n        images, img_masks, lang_tokens, lang_masks, state = (\n            self._preprocess_observation(observation, train=False)\n        )\n        # move device\n        device = next(self.parameters()).device\n        images = [img.to(device) for img in images]\n        img_masks = [m.to(device) for m in img_masks]\n        state = state.to(device)\n        # nft inputs\n        nft_inputs = kwargs[\"nft_inputs\"]\n        x_t = nft_inputs[\"x_t\"].to(device)\n        t = nft_inputs[\"timesteps\"].to(device)\n        # get v_theta\n        _, prefix_pad_masks, past_key_values = self._build_prefix_cache(\n            images, img_masks, lang_tokens, lang_masks\n        )\n        compute_values = kwargs.get(\"compute_values\", False)\n        v_theta, suffix_out = self.get_velocity(\n            state, x_t, t, prefix_pad_masks, past_key_values\n        )\n        v_theta = v_theta[:, : self.config.action_chunk, :]\n        # result\n        result: dict[str, Any] = {\"v_theta\": v_theta, \"x_t\": x_t, \"timesteps\": t}\n        if compute_values and self.config.add_value_head:\n            result[\"values\"] = self._compute_value_from_suffix(suffix_out)[:, None]\n        return result\n\n    def obs_processor(self, env_obs):\n        env_states = self._select_configured_state(env_obs[\"states\"])\n        processed_obs = {\n            \"observation/image\": env_obs[\"main_images\"],\n            \"prompt\": env_obs[\"task_descriptions\"],\n        }\n        if \"calvin\" in self.config.config_name:\n            state = env_states\n            processed_obs[\"observation/state_ee_pos\"] = state[:, :3]\n            processed_obs[\"observation/state_ee_rot\"] = state[:, 3:6]\n            processed_obs[\"observation/state_gripper\"] = state[:, 6:7]\n        else:\n            processed_obs[\"observation/state\"] = env_states\n        if env_obs[\"wrist_images\"] is not None:\n            processed_obs[\"observation/wrist_image\"] = env_obs[\"wrist_images\"]\n        if env_obs[\"extra_view_images\"] is not None:\n            processed_obs[\"observation/extra_view_image\"] = env_obs[\"extra_view_images\"]\n        return processed_obs\n\n    def precision_processor(self, processed_obs):\n        device = next(self.parameters()).device\n        for key, value in processed_obs.items():\n            if isinstance(value, list):\n                processed_obs[key] = [\n                    item.to(device=device).contiguous()\n                    if torch.is_tensor(item)\n                    else item\n                    for item in value\n                ]\n            elif torch.is_tensor(value):\n                processed_obs[key] = value.to(device=device).contiguous()\n            elif isinstance(value, dict):\n                for sub_key, sub_value in value.items():\n                    processed_obs[key][sub_key] = sub_value.to(\n                        device=device\n                    ).contiguous()\n        return processed_obs\n\n    def predict_action_batch(\n        self,\n        env_obs,\n        mode: Literal[\"train\", \"eval\"] = \"train\",\n        compute_values=True,\n        rtc_context: RTCGuidanceContext | None = None,\n        return_dvac_telemetry: bool = False,\n        **kwargs,\n    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, dict[str, Any]]:\n        to_process_obs = self.obs_processor(env_obs)  # env obs -> policy input obs\n        processed_obs = self.input_transform(\n            to_process_obs, transpose=False\n        )  # policy input obs -> model input obs\n        processed_obs = self.precision_processor(\n            processed_obs\n        )  # obs precision processor\n        observation = _model.Observation.from_dict(processed_obs)\n\n        is_dsrl_active = self.config.use_dsrl\n        if is_dsrl_active:\n            # DSRL mode (both train and eval)\n\n            # Step 1: SAC agent outputs noise\n            dsrl_obs = {\"images\": [env_obs[\"main_images\"]], \"states\": env_obs[\"states\"]}\n\n            noise_actions, noise_logprob, _ = self.sac_forward(\n                dsrl_obs, train=False, mode=mode\n            )\n\n            # Step 2: Use noise to sample actual actions from diffusion model\n            outputs = self.sample_actions(\n                observation,\n                noise=noise_actions,\n                mode=\"eval\",\n                compute_values=compute_values,\n                return_dvac_telemetry=return_dvac_telemetry,\n            )\n\n            # Step 3: Extract actual actions for environment interaction\n            real_actions = self.output_transform(\n                {\"actions\": outputs[\"actions\"], \"state\": observation.state}\n            )[\"actions\"]\n\n            # Return actual actions to environment, but forward_inputs stores noise.\n            actions = real_actions\n            prev_logprobs = noise_logprob  # SAC noise logprob\n            prev_values = outputs.get(\"prev_values\")\n            forward_action = noise_actions  # Used for SAC training\n\n        else:\n            # Non-DSRL or eval mode\n            if rtc_context is not None and mode == \"eval\" and self.config.rtc_enabled:\n                if return_dvac_telemetry:\n                    raise ValueError(\n                        \"DVAC telemetry records the native OpenPI sampler; RTC guidance \"\n                        \"telemetry is not supported.\"\n                    )\n                # RTC is only used during evaluation: the rollout worker passes\n                # the previous model-space action chunk so the sampler can guide\n                # the overlapping horizon without changing normal training.\n                outputs = self.sample_actions_with_rtc_guidance(\n                    observation,\n                    rtc_context=rtc_context,\n                    mode=mode,\n                    compute_values=compute_values,\n                )\n            else:\n                outputs = self.sample_actions(\n                    observation,\n                    mode=mode,\n                    compute_values=compute_values,\n                    return_dvac_telemetry=return_dvac_telemetry,\n                )\n            actions = self.output_transform(\n                {\"actions\": outputs[\"actions\"], \"state\": observation.state}\n            )[\"actions\"]\n            prev_logprobs = outputs[\"prev_logprobs\"]\n            prev_values = outputs[\"prev_values\"]\n            forward_action = None\n\n        forward_inputs = {\n            \"chains\": outputs[\"chains\"],\n            \"denoise_inds\": outputs[\"denoise_inds\"],\n            \"tokenized_prompt\": processed_obs[\"tokenized_prompt\"],\n            \"tokenized_prompt_mask\": processed_obs[\"tokenized_prompt_mask\"],\n            # \"action\" is the env-executed action, and \"model_action\" is the original output by the model.\n            # For small models, they are consistent. For large models (like pi), \"action\" is the result after output_transform.\n            # For realworld human-in-the-loop training, only \"action\" can be provided by human.\n            \"action\": actions.reshape(actions.shape[0], -1).contiguous(),\n            \"model_action\": outputs[\"actions\"]\n            .reshape(outputs[\"actions\"].shape[0], -1)\n            .contiguous(),\n        }\n        if forward_action is not None:\n            forward_inputs[\"action\"] = forward_action\n\n        if self.config.is_nft:\n            nft_outputs = {\n                key: value for key, value in outputs.items() if key.startswith(\"nft_\")\n            }\n            forward_inputs.update(nft_outputs)\n\n        # Clone observations to avoid cross-step reference issues.\n        cloned_obs = copy_dict_tensor(\n            {k: v for k, v in to_process_obs.items() if k != \"prompt\"}\n        )\n        forward_inputs.update(cloned_obs)\n\n        result = {\n            \"prev_logprobs\": prev_logprobs,\n            \"prev_values\": prev_values,\n            \"forward_inputs\": forward_inputs,\n            \"model_actions\": outputs[\"actions\"],\n        }\n        if return_dvac_telemetry:\n            result[\"dvac_telemetry\"] = outputs[\"dvac_telemetry\"]\n        return actions, result\n\n    @torch.no_grad()\n    def sample_actions_with_rtc_guidance(\n        self,\n        observation: _model.Observation,\n        rtc_context: RTCGuidanceContext,\n        noise=None,\n        mode=\"eval\",\n        compute_values=True,\n    ) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:\n        \"\"\"Sample OpenPI actions with RTC overlap guidance enabled.\"\"\"\n        if self.config.rtc_guidance_mode != \"approx\":\n            raise NotImplementedError(\n                f\"Unsupported RTC guidance mode: {self.config.rtc_guidance_mode}\"\n            )\n        return _sample_actions_with_rtc_guidance(\n            self,\n            observation,\n            rtc_context=rtc_context,\n            noise=noise,\n            mode=mode,\n            compute_values=compute_values,\n        )\n\n    @torch.no_grad()\n    def sample_actions(\n        self,\n        observation: _model.Observation,\n        noise=None,\n        mode=\"train\",\n        compute_values=True,\n        return_dvac_telemetry: bool = False,\n    ) -> torch.Tensor:\n        \"\"\"Do a full inference forward and compute the action (batch_size x num_steps x num_motors)\"\"\"\n        bsize = observation.state.shape[0]\n        device = observation.state.device\n        if noise is None:\n            actions_shape = (bsize, self.config.action_horizon, self.config.action_dim)\n            noise = self.sample_noise(actions_shape, device)\n        else:\n            # DSRL: SAC provides noise, convert dtype to match action_in_proj\n            noise = noise.to(self.action_in_proj.weight.dtype)\n\n        images, img_masks, lang_tokens, lang_masks, state = (\n            self._preprocess_observation(observation, train=False)\n        )\n\n        prefix_output, prefix_pad_masks, past_key_values = self._build_prefix_cache(\n            images, img_masks, lang_tokens, lang_masks\n        )\n\n        return self._sample_actions_with_prefix_cache(\n            state,\n            prefix_output,\n            prefix_pad_masks,\n            past_key_values,\n            noise=noise,\n            mode=mode,\n            compute_values=compute_values,\n            return_dvac_telemetry=return_dvac_telemetry,\n        )\n\n    def _sample_actions_with_prefix_cache(\n        self,\n        state,\n        prefix_output,\n        prefix_pad_masks,\n        past_key_values,\n        noise=None,\n        mode=\"train\",\n        compute_values=True,\n        return_dvac_telemetry: bool = False,\n    ) -> torch.Tensor:\n        bsize = state.shape[0]\n        device = state.device\n        num_steps = self.config.num_steps\n        if noise is None:\n            actions_shape = (bsize, self.config.action_horizon, self.config.action_dim)\n            noise = self.sample_noise(actions_shape, device)\n        else:\n            # DSRL: SAC provides noise, convert dtype to match action_in_proj\n            noise = noise.to(self.action_in_proj.weight.dtype)\n\n        x_t = noise\n        # add sde sample and traj collect\n        chains = []\n        log_probs = []\n        values = []\n        chains.append(x_t)\n        endpoint_trace = [] if return_dvac_telemetry else None\n        telemetry_timesteps = (\n            self._get_timesteps(num_steps, device) if return_dvac_telemetry else None\n        )\n\n        # add value based on the vlm for pi05, expert for pi0\n        if self.use_vlm_value:\n            values_vlm = self.get_value_from_vlm(prefix_output)\n        if self.config.joint_logprob:\n            initial_log_prob = self.get_logprob_norm(\n                x_t, torch.zeros_like(noise), torch.ones_like(noise)\n            )\n            log_probs.append(initial_log_prob)\n\n        # In the joint logprob mode, we need to sample the logprob for each denoise step\n        # In the non-joint logprob mode, only one denoise step is sampled and ode-sde mix sampling is used\n        # denoise index\n        collect_nft_state = self.config.is_nft and mode == \"train\"\n        if mode == \"train\":\n            if self.config.joint_logprob or collect_nft_state:\n                denoise_inds = torch.arange(num_steps)\n            else:\n                if self.config.ignore_last:\n                    denoise_inds = torch.tensor(\n                        [random.randint(0, num_steps - 2)] * num_steps\n                    )\n                else:\n                    denoise_inds = torch.tensor(\n                        [random.randint(0, num_steps - 1)] * num_steps\n                    )\n        else:\n            denoise_inds = torch.tensor([-1] * num_steps)\n        denoise_inds = denoise_inds[None].repeat(bsize, 1)\n\n        # collect nft states for nft algorithm\n        nft_state = self._init_nft_state(collect_nft_state, x_t, num_steps, device)\n\n        # denoise step\n        for idx in range(num_steps):\n            # sample mean var val\n            if idx == denoise_inds[0][idx]:\n                sample_method = self.config.noise_method\n            else:\n                sample_method = \"flow_ode\"\n            x_t_prev = x_t\n            x_t_mean, x_t_std, value_t, v_t = self.sample_mean_var_val(\n                x_t,\n                idx,\n                state,\n                prefix_pad_masks,\n                past_key_values,\n                sample_method,\n                num_steps,\n                compute_values,\n            )\n            if endpoint_trace is not None:\n                t_i = telemetry_timesteps[idx].to(dtype=x_t_prev.dtype)\n                endpoint_trace.append(\n                    (x_t_prev - v_t * t_i)[..., : self.config.action_env_dim].detach()\n                )\n            # Euler step - use new tensor assignment instead of in-place operation\n            x_t = x_t_mean + self.sample_noise(x_t.shape, device) * x_t_std\n            self._update_nft_state(nft_state, idx, x_t_prev, v_t, x_t, sample_method)\n            log_prob = self.get_logprob_norm(x_t, x_t_mean, x_t_std)\n            # store\n            values.append(value_t)\n            chains.append(x_t)\n            log_probs.append(log_prob)\n        x_0 = x_t\n        chains = torch.stack(chains, dim=1)\n        # post process for logprob\n        log_probs = torch.stack(log_probs, dim=1)[\n            :, :, : self.config.action_chunk, : self.config.action_env_dim\n        ]\n        if self.config.joint_logprob:\n            log_probs = log_probs.mean(dim=1)\n        else:\n            log_probs = log_probs[\n                torch.arange(log_probs.shape[0], device=device),\n                denoise_inds[:, 0],\n            ]\n        # post process for value\n        if self.use_vlm_value:\n            values = values_vlm[:, None]\n        else:\n            values = torch.stack(values, dim=1).mean(dim=-1, keepdim=True)\n        result = {\n            \"actions\": x_0,\n            \"chains\": chains,\n            \"prev_logprobs\": log_probs,\n            \"prev_values\": values,\n            \"denoise_inds\": denoise_inds,\n        }\n        if collect_nft_state:\n            result.update(nft_state)\n            result[\"nft_x0\"] = x_0.detach()\n        if endpoint_trace is not None:\n            result[\"dvac_telemetry\"] = {\n                \"x_chain\": chains[..., : self.config.action_env_dim].detach(),\n                \"z_endpoint\": torch.stack(endpoint_trace, dim=1),\n                \"timesteps\": telemetry_timesteps[:-1].detach(),\n                \"final_model_action\": x_0[..., : self.config.action_env_dim].detach(),\n            }\n        return result\n\n    def _get_timesteps(self, denoise_steps, device):\n        timesteps = torch.linspace(1, 1 / denoise_steps, denoise_steps, device=device)\n        timesteps = torch.cat([timesteps, torch.zeros((1), device=device)])\n        return timesteps\n\n    def sample_mean_var_val(\n        self,\n        x_t,\n        idx,\n        state,\n        prefix_pad_masks,\n        past_key_values,\n        sample_method,\n        denoise_steps,\n        compute_values=True,\n    ):\n        \"\"\"\n        Sample the mean, variance and value of the action at a given timestep.\n        Rollout sample (idx is int) and actor get_log_prob_value (idx is tensor)\n        will load this function. `sample_method` is one of flow_ode/flow_sde/\n        flow_cps/flow_noise.\n        \"\"\"\n        # expand the shape\n        bsize = state.shape[0]\n        device = state.device\n        if isinstance(idx, int):\n            idx = torch.full((), idx, device=device).expand(bsize)\n        # build parameters\n        noise_level = self._get_noise_level(device=device, dtype=x_t.dtype)\n        timesteps = self._get_timesteps(denoise_steps, device)\n        # input parameters\n        t_input = timesteps[idx]\n        delta = timesteps[idx] - timesteps[idx + 1]\n        # velocity prediction\n        v_t, suffix_out = self.get_velocity(\n            state, x_t, t_input, prefix_pad_masks, past_key_values\n        )\n        # value prediction\n        if (\n            self.config.add_value_head\n            and compute_values\n            and not self.config.value_after_vlm\n        ):\n            value_t = self._compute_value_from_suffix(suffix_out)\n        else:\n            value_t = torch.zeros((bsize), device=device)\n        # sample mean and variance\n        delta = delta[:, None, None].expand_as(x_t)\n        t_input = t_input[:, None, None].expand_as(x_t)\n        x0_pred = x_t - v_t * t_input\n        x1_pred = x_t + v_t * (1 - t_input)\n\n        if sample_method == \"flow_ode\":\n            x0_weight = 1 - (t_input - delta)\n            x1_weight = t_input - delta\n            x_t_std = torch.zeros_like(t_input)\n        elif sample_method == \"flow_sde\":\n            denom_timesteps = torch.where(timesteps == 1, timesteps[1], timesteps)\n            sigma_ratio = timesteps / (1 - denom_timesteps)\n            sigmas = noise_level * torch.sqrt(sigma_ratio)[:-1]\n            sigma_i = sigmas[idx][:, None, None].expand_as(x_t)\n            x0_weight = torch.ones_like(t_input) - (t_input - delta)\n            x1_weight = t_input - delta - sigma_i**2 * delta / (2 * t_input)\n            x_t_std = torch.sqrt(delta) * sigma_i\n        elif sample_method == \"flow_cps\":\n            pi = torch.pi\n            cos_term = torch.cos(pi * noise_level / 2).to(device)\n            sin_term = torch.sin(pi * noise_level / 2).to(device)\n            x0_weight = torch.ones_like(t_input) - (t_input - delta)\n            x1_weight = (t_input - delta) * cos_term\n            x_t_std = (t_input - delta) * sin_term\n        elif sample_method == \"flow_noise\":\n            x0_weight = 1 - (t_input - delta)\n            x1_weight = t_input - delta\n            x_t_std = self.noise_head(suffix_out)\n        else:\n            raise ValueError(f\"Invalid noise method: {sample_method}\")\n        x_t_mean = x0_pred * x0_weight + x1_pred * x1_weight\n        return x_t_mean, x_t_std, value_t, v_t\n\n    def get_suffix_out(\n        self,\n        state,\n        prefix_pad_masks,\n        past_key_values,\n        x_t,\n        timestep,\n    ):\n        \"\"\"Apply one denoising step of the noise `x_t` at a given timestep.\"\"\"\n        suffix_embs, suffix_pad_masks, suffix_att_masks, adarms_cond = (\n            self.embed_suffix(state, x_t, timestep)\n        )\n\n        suffix_len = suffix_pad_masks.shape[1]\n        batch_size = prefix_pad_masks.shape[0]\n        prefix_len = prefix_pad_masks.shape[1]\n\n        prefix_pad_2d_masks = prefix_pad_masks[:, None, :].expand(\n            batch_size, suffix_len, prefix_len\n        )\n\n        suffix_att_2d_masks = make_att_2d_masks(suffix_pad_masks, suffix_att_masks)\n\n        full_att_2d_masks = torch.cat([prefix_pad_2d_masks, suffix_att_2d_masks], dim=2)\n\n        prefix_offsets = torch.sum(prefix_pad_masks, dim=-1)[:, None]\n        position_ids = prefix_offsets + torch.cumsum(suffix_pad_masks, dim=1) - 1\n\n        # Prepare attention masks\n        full_att_2d_masks_4d = self._prepare_attention_masks_4d(full_att_2d_masks)\n        self.paligemma_with_expert.gemma_expert.model.config._attn_implementation = (\n            \"eager\"  # noqa: SLF001\n        )\n\n        outputs_embeds, _ = self.paligemma_with_expert.forward(\n            attention_mask=full_att_2d_masks_4d,\n            position_ids=position_ids,\n            past_key_values=past_key_values,\n            inputs_embeds=[None, suffix_embs],\n            use_cache=False,\n            adarms_cond=[None, adarms_cond],\n        )\n\n        suffix_out = outputs_embeds[1]\n        suffix_out = suffix_out[:, -self.config.action_horizon :]\n        suffix_out = suffix_out.to(dtype=torch.float32)\n        return suffix_out\n\n    def get_velocity(self, state, x_t, timestep, prefix_pad_masks, past_key_values):\n        \"\"\"Compute velocity prediction v_t and raw suffix_out at a given timestep.\"\"\"\n        suffix_out = self.get_suffix_out(\n            state, prefix_pad_masks, past_key_values, x_t, timestep\n        )\n        v_t = self.action_out_proj(suffix_out)\n        return v_t, suffix_out\n\n    def _build_prefix_cache(self, images, img_masks, lang_tokens, lang_masks):\n        \"\"\"Embed prefix tokens and compute KV cache for efficient suffix generation.\"\"\"\n        prefix_embs, prefix_pad_masks, prefix_att_masks = self.embed_prefix(\n            images, img_masks, lang_tokens, lang_masks\n        )\n        prefix_att_2d_masks = make_att_2d_masks(prefix_pad_masks, prefix_att_masks)\n        prefix_position_ids = torch.cumsum(prefix_pad_masks, dim=1) - 1\n        prefix_att_2d_masks_4d = self._prepare_attention_masks_4d(prefix_att_2d_masks)\n        self.paligemma_with_expert.paligemma.language_model.config._attn_implementation = \"eager\"  # noqa: SLF001\n        (prefix_output, _), past_key_values = self.paligemma_with_expert.forward(\n            attention_mask=prefix_att_2d_masks_4d,\n            position_ids=prefix_position_ids,\n            past_key_values=None,\n            inputs_embeds=[prefix_embs, None],\n            use_cache=True,\n        )\n        return prefix_output, prefix_pad_masks, past_key_values\n\n    def _compute_value_from_suffix(self, suffix_out):\n        \"\"\"Compute value from suffix output using value head.\"\"\"\n        if self.config.chunk_critic_input:\n            suffix_out_value = torch.mean(\n                suffix_out[:, : self.config.action_chunk], dim=1, keepdim=False\n            )\n        else:\n            suffix_out_value = torch.mean(suffix_out, dim=1, keepdim=False)\n        if self.config.detach_critic_input:\n            suffix_out_value = suffix_out_value.detach()\n        return self.value_head(suffix_out_value)[:, 0]\n\n    # TODO: to check potential nan here\n    def get_logprob_norm(self, sample, mu, sigma):\n        # logprob = log p(x|mu,sigma) = -log(sigma) - 0.5 * log(2 * pi) - 0.5 * ((x - mu) / sigma) ** 2\n        if self.config.safe_get_logprob:\n            log_prob = -torch.pow((sample - mu), 2)\n        else:\n            mask = sigma == 0\n            sigma_safe = torch.where(mask, torch.ones_like(sigma), sigma)\n            constant_term = -torch.log(sigma_safe) - 0.5 * torch.log(\n                2 * torch.pi * torch.ones_like(sample)\n            )\n            exponent_term = -0.5 * torch.pow((sample - mu) / sigma_safe, 2)\n            log_prob = constant_term + exponent_term\n            log_prob = torch.where(mask, torch.zeros_like(log_prob), log_prob)\n        return log_prob\n\n    def preprocess_for_train(self, data):\n        return data\n\n    def get_log_prob_value(\n        self,\n        images,\n        img_masks,\n        lang_tokens,\n        lang_masks,\n        state,\n        chains,\n        denoise_inds,\n        compute_values=False,\n    ):\n        bsize = state.shape[0]\n        batch_indices = torch.arange(bsize)\n        prefix_output, prefix_pad_masks, past_key_values = self._build_prefix_cache(\n            images, img_masks, lang_tokens, lang_masks\n        )\n        chains_log_probs = []\n        chains_values = []\n        chains_entropy = []\n\n        # get log prob\n        if self.config.joint_logprob:\n            num_steps = self.config.num_steps\n            initial_log_prob = self.get_logprob_norm(\n                chains[:, 0],\n                torch.zeros_like(chains[:, 0]),\n                torch.ones_like(chains[:, 0]),\n            )\n            initial_entropy = self.gaussian_entropy(torch.ones_like(chains[:, 0]))\n            chains_log_probs.append(initial_log_prob)\n            chains_entropy.append(initial_entropy)\n        else:\n            num_steps = 1\n        for idx in range(num_steps):\n            denoise_ind = denoise_inds[:, idx]\n            chains_pre = chains[batch_indices, denoise_ind]\n            chains_next = chains[batch_indices, denoise_ind + 1]\n            x_t_mean, x_t_std, value_t, _ = self.sample_mean_var_val(\n                chains_pre,\n                denoise_ind,\n                state,\n                prefix_pad_masks,\n                past_key_values,\n                self.config.noise_method,\n                self.config.num_steps,\n                compute_values,\n            )\n            log_probs = self.get_logprob_norm(chains_next, x_t_mean, x_t_std)\n            entropy = self.gaussian_entropy(x_t_std)\n            chains_log_probs.append(log_probs)\n            chains_entropy.append(entropy)\n            if not self.use_vlm_value:\n                chains_values.append(value_t)\n        if self.use_vlm_value:\n            chains_values.append(self.get_value_from_vlm(prefix_output))\n        chains_log_probs = torch.stack(chains_log_probs, dim=1)\n        chains_values = torch.stack(chains_values, dim=1)\n\n        # entropy is only available for flow-noise method\n        if self.config.noise_method == \"flow_noise\":\n            chains_entropy = torch.stack(chains_entropy, dim=1)\n        else:\n            chains_entropy = torch.zeros_like(chains_log_probs)\n        return chains_log_probs, chains_values, chains_entropy\n\n    def get_value_from_vlm(self, prefix_output):\n        # prefix_output:\n        # pi05: [bs, (256 * 3 + 200) = 968, 2048]\n        # pi0: [bs, (256 * 3 + 48) = 816, 1024]\n        # token length\n        if \"pi05_\" in self.config.config_name:\n            lang_token_len = 200\n            all_token_length = 968\n        elif \"pi0_\" in self.config.config_name:\n            lang_token_len = 48\n            all_token_length = 816\n\n        if self.config.value_vlm_mode == \"mean_token\":\n            prefix_mask = (\n                [True] * 256 * self.config.num_images_in_input\n                + [False] * 256 * (3 - self.config.num_images_in_input)\n                + [True] * lang_token_len\n            )\n        elif self.config.value_vlm_mode == \"last_token\":\n            prefix_mask = [False] * (all_token_length - 1) + [True] * 1\n        elif self.config.value_vlm_mode == \"first_token\":\n            prefix_mask = [True] * 1 + [False] * (all_token_length - 1)\n        prefix_out_value = prefix_output[:, prefix_mask, :]\n        prefix_out_value = prefix_out_value.mean(dim=1, keepdim=False)\n        prefix_out_value = prefix_out_value.to(dtype=torch.float32)\n        values_vlm = self.value_head(prefix_out_value)[:, 0]\n        return values_vlm\n\n    def gaussian_entropy(self, sigma):\n        mask = sigma == 0\n        sigma_safe = torch.where(mask, torch.ones_like(sigma), sigma)\n        entropy = 0.5 * torch.log(2 * math.pi * math.e * (sigma_safe**2))\n        return entropy\n\n    def freeze_vlm(self):\n        if self.config.train_expert_only:\n            # Base freeze: paligemma (SigLIP vision encoder + Gemma)\n            self.paligemma_with_expert.paligemma.eval()\n            for params in self.paligemma_with_expert.paligemma.parameters():\n                params.requires_grad = False\n\n            # ========== DSRL additional freezing ==========\n            if self.config.use_dsrl:\n                self.logger.info(\n                    \"[FREEZE_VLM] DSRL mode: freezing gemma_expert parameters\"\n                )\n                self.paligemma_with_expert.gemma_expert.eval()\n                for params in self.paligemma_with_expert.gemma_expert.parameters():\n                    params.requires_grad = False\n\n                # Freeze projection layers (used in rollout/eval but not optimized).\n                # Pi0 has: action_in_proj, action_out_proj, state_proj, action_time_mlp_in/out\n                # Pi0.5 has: action_in_proj, action_out_proj, time_mlp_in/out (no state_proj)\n                self.logger.info(\n                    \"[FREEZE_VLM] DSRL mode: freezing projection layers (used in rollout/eval but not optimized)\"\n                )\n                if self.pi05:\n                    projection_names = [\n                        \"action_in_proj\",\n                        \"action_out_proj\",\n                        \"time_mlp_in\",\n                        \"time_mlp_out\",\n                    ]\n                else:\n                    projection_names = [\n                        \"action_in_proj\",\n                        \"action_out_proj\",\n                        \"state_proj\",\n                        \"action_time_mlp\",\n                    ]\n                frozen_count = 0\n                for name, param in self.named_parameters():\n                    if any(proj_name in name for proj_name in projection_names):\n                        param.requires_grad = False\n                        frozen_count += 1\n                        if frozen_count <= 10:  # Print first 10 for brevity\n                            self.logger.info(f\"  Froze: {name}\")\n                if frozen_count > 10:\n                    self.logger.info(\n                        f\"  ... and {frozen_count - 10} more projection layer parameters\"\n                    )\n\n                # Freeze reinflow_explore_noise_net (only used in reinflow diffuser sampling)\n                if hasattr(self, \"reinflow_explore_noise_net\"):\n                    self.logger.info(\n                        \"[FREEZE_VLM] DSRL mode: freezing reinflow_explore_noise_net (used in non-DSRL rollout but not optimized)\"\n                    )\n                    self.reinflow_explore_noise_net.eval()\n                    noise_net_params = 0\n                    for params in self.reinflow_explore_noise_net.parameters():\n                        params.requires_grad = False\n                        noise_net_params += params.numel()\n                    self.logger.info(\n                        f\"  Froze {noise_net_params:,} parameters in reinflow_explore_noise_net\"\n                    )\n\n    # ===== DSRL-specific methods =====\n\n    def sac_forward(\n        self, obs=None, data=None, train=False, return_dist_params=False, **kwargs\n    ):\n        \"\"\"SAC forward pass for DSRL.\n\n        Args:\n            obs: Observation dict (preferred, matches sac_dsrl).\n                 Supports two formats:\n                   1. {\"images\": list of tensors, \"states\": tensor} - internal format\n                   2. {\"main_images\": tensor, \"wrist_images\": tensor, \"states\": tensor} - env format\n            data: Dictionary containing observations (legacy, for backward compatibility).\n            train: Whether to use data augmentation.\n            return_dist_params: Whether to return distribution parameters for logging.\n\n        Returns:\n            actions: [B, action_horizon, output_dim] - noise or actual actions\n            logprobs: [B] - log probabilities\n            dist_params: (mean, std) or None - distribution parameters for logging\n        \"\"\"\n        if not self.config.use_dsrl:\n            raise ValueError(\"sac_forward called but use_dsrl=False\")\n\n        # Support both call styles: obs (new, from sac_dsrl) or data (legacy)\n        if obs is None:\n            obs = data.get(\"obs\", data) if data is not None else kwargs.get(\"obs\", {})\n\n        # Handle two obs formats:\n        # Format 1 (internal): {\"images\": [...], \"states\": ...}\n        # Format 2 (env): {\"main_images\": ..., \"wrist_images\": ..., \"states\": ...}\n        if \"images\" not in obs:\n            # Convert env format to internal format\n            if \"main_images\" in obs:\n                obs = {\"images\": [obs[\"main_images\"]], \"states\": obs[\"states\"]}\n            else:\n                raise ValueError(\n                    f\"Invalid obs format: {obs.keys()}. Expected 'images' or 'main_images' key.\"\n                )\n\n        # Preprocess images: resize to 64x64, use only agentview camera\n        # Returns [B, 1, C, 64, 64] in [-1, 1] range (float32)\n        images = self._preprocess_dsrl_images(obs[\"images\"], train=train)\n        states = self._preprocess_states(obs[\"states\"])\n\n        # Move to the same device as actor encoders, convert to bfloat16\n        device = next(self.actor_image_encoder.parameters()).device\n        images = images.to(device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n        states = states.to(device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n\n        # Extract features (using actor's independent encoder)\n        image_features = self.actor_image_encoder(images)  # [B, 64]\n        state_features = self.actor_state_encoder(states)  # [B, 64]\n        features = torch.cat([state_features, image_features], dim=-1)  # [B, 128]\n\n        # Sample from GaussianPolicy\n        mode = kwargs.get(\"mode\", \"train\")\n        deterministic = mode == \"eval\"\n\n        action_noise, logprobs = self.dsrl_action_noise_net.sample(\n            features, deterministic=deterministic\n        )\n\n        # Optional: return distribution parameters for logging\n        dist_params = None\n        if return_dist_params:\n            dist = self.dsrl_action_noise_net.forward(features)\n            dist_params = (dist.mean, dist.stddev)\n\n        return action_noise, logprobs, dist_params\n\n    def sac_q_forward(\n        self,\n        obs=None,\n        data=None,\n        actions=None,\n        detach_encoder=False,\n        train=False,\n        **kwargs,\n    ):\n        \"\"\"Q-value forward pass for DSRL.\n\n        Args:\n            obs: Observation dict (preferred, matches sac_dsrl).\n                 Supports two formats:\n                   1. {\"images\": list of tensors, \"states\": tensor} - internal format\n                   2. {\"main_images\": tensor, \"wrist_images\": tensor, \"states\": tensor} - env format\n            data: Dictionary containing observations (legacy, for backward compatibility).\n            actions: [B, action_dim] or [B, action_horizon, action_dim]\n            detach_encoder: Whether to detach encoder gradients.\n            train: Whether to use data augmentation.\n\n        Returns:\n            q_values: [B, num_q_heads] - Q-values from all Q-networks.\n        \"\"\"\n        if not self.config.use_dsrl:\n            raise ValueError(\"sac_q_forward called but use_dsrl=False\")\n\n        # Support both call styles: obs (new, from sac_dsrl) or data (legacy)\n        if obs is None:\n            obs = data.get(\"obs\", data) if data is not None else kwargs.get(\"obs\", {})\n        if actions is None:\n            actions = kwargs.get(\"actions\")\n\n        # Handle two obs formats:\n        # Format 1 (internal): {\"images\": [...], \"states\": ...}\n        # Format 2 (env): {\"main_images\": ..., \"wrist_images\": ..., \"states\": ...}\n        if \"images\" not in obs:\n            # Convert env format to internal format\n            if \"main_images\" in obs:\n                obs = {\"images\": [obs[\"main_images\"]], \"states\": obs[\"states\"]}\n            else:\n                raise ValueError(\n                    f\"Invalid obs format: {obs.keys()}. Expected 'images' or 'main_images' key.\"\n                )\n\n        # Preprocess images: resize to 64x64, use only agentview camera\n        # Returns [B, 1, C, 64, 64] in [-1, 1] range (float32)\n        images = self._preprocess_dsrl_images(obs[\"images\"], train=train)\n        states = self._preprocess_states(obs[\"states\"])\n\n        # Move to the same device as critic encoders, convert to bfloat16\n        device = next(self.critic_image_encoder.parameters()).device\n        images = images.to(device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n        states = states.to(device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n        actions = actions.to(device=device, dtype=torch.bfloat16)\n\n        # Extract features (using critic's independent encoder)\n        image_features = self.critic_image_encoder(images)\n        state_features = self.critic_state_encoder(states)\n\n        # Optionally detach encoder\n        if detach_encoder:\n            image_features = image_features.detach()\n            state_features = state_features.detach()\n\n        # Process actions (DSRL: should be noise, already flattened)\n        if actions.dim() == 3:\n            actions = actions[:, 0, :]  # [B, action_horizon, dim] -> [B, dim]\n\n        # Compute Q values\n        q_values = self.q_head(state_features, image_features, actions)\n\n        return q_values\n\n    # ===== NFT-specific methods =====\n\n    def _init_nft_state(\n        self,\n        collect_nft_state: bool,\n        x_t: torch.Tensor,\n        num_steps: int,\n        device: torch.device,\n    ) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor] | None:\n        \"\"\"Initialize NFT state buffers for rollout sampling.\"\"\"\n        if not collect_nft_state:\n            return None\n        return {\n            \"nft_step_index\": torch.randint(\n                0, num_steps, (x_t.shape[0],), device=device\n            ),\n            \"nft_xcur\": torch.zeros_like(x_t),\n            \"nft_v\": torch.zeros_like(x_t),\n            \"nft_xnext\": torch.zeros_like(x_t),\n            \"nft_noise_level\": torch.zeros(\n                x_t.shape[0], device=device, dtype=x_t.dtype\n            ),\n        }\n\n    def _update_nft_state(\n        self,\n        nft_state: dict[str, torch.Tensor] | None,\n        idx: int,\n        x_t_prev: torch.Tensor,\n        v_t: torch.Tensor,\n        x_t: torch.Tensor,\n        sample_method: str,\n    ) -> None:\n        \"\"\"Update NFT state buffers for the selected denoising step.\"\"\"\n        if nft_state is None:\n            return\n        mask = nft_state[\"nft_step_index\"] == idx\n        if not mask.any():\n            return\n        mask_bc = mask[:, None, None]\n        nft_state[\"nft_xcur\"] = torch.where(\n            mask_bc, x_t_prev.detach(), nft_state[\"nft_xcur\"]\n        )\n        nft_state[\"nft_v\"] = torch.where(mask_bc, v_t.detach(), nft_state[\"nft_v\"])\n        nft_state[\"nft_xnext\"] = torch.where(\n            mask_bc, x_t.detach(), nft_state[\"nft_xnext\"]\n        )\n        noise_level = self._get_noise_level(\n            device=x_t.device, dtype=x_t.dtype, sample_method=sample_method\n        )\n        nft_state[\"nft_noise_level\"] = torch.where(\n            mask,\n            torch.full_like(nft_state[\"nft_noise_level\"], float(noise_level.item())),\n            nft_state[\"nft_noise_level\"],\n        )\n\n    def _get_noise_level(\n        self, device: torch.device, dtype: torch.dtype, sample_method: str | None = None\n    ) -> torch.Tensor:\n        method = sample_method or self.config.noise_method\n        if method == \"flow_ode\":\n            return torch.zeros((), device=device, dtype=dtype)\n        if self.config.noise_anneal:\n            noise_start, noise_end, anneal_steps = self.config.noise_params\n            noise_level = (\n                noise_start\n                + (noise_end - noise_start)\n                * min(self.global_step, anneal_steps)\n                / anneal_steps\n            )\n        else:\n            noise_level = self.config.noise_level\n        return torch.full((), noise_level, device=device, dtype=dtype)\n\n    def _preprocess_dsrl_images(self, images, train=False):\n        \"\"\"Preprocess images for DSRL: resize to 64x64, use only agentview camera.\n\n        Args:\n            images: List of tensors.\n                Can be [B, H, W, C] (NHWC) from environment or\n                [B, C, H, W] (NCHW) from processed data.\n                For Libero: images[0] is agentview, images[1] is wrist.\n            train: Whether to use data augmentation (placeholder for now).\n\n        Returns:\n            Tensor of shape [B, 1, C, 64, 64] - only agentview, resized, in [-1, 1].\n        \"\"\"\n\n        # Extract only agentview camera (first image in the list)\n        if isinstance(images, list):\n            agentview_img = images[0]\n        else:\n            # Assume it's already a tensor\n            agentview_img = images\n\n        # Detect and convert NHWC -> NCHW (environment outputs NHWC)\n        if agentview_img.shape[-1] == 3:\n            # NHWC format: [B, H, W, C] -> [B, C, H, W]\n            agentview_img = agentview_img.permute(0, 3, 1, 2)\n\n        B, C, H, W = agentview_img.shape\n        target_size = 64\n\n        # ===== UNIFIED VALUE RANGE HANDLING =====\n        # Convert to float32 and normalize to [0, 1] for PyTorch resize\n        if agentview_img.dtype == torch.uint8:\n            # [0, 255] -> [0, 1]\n            agentview_img = agentview_img.float() / 255.0\n        else:\n            # Check if in [-1, 1] range\n            if agentview_img.min() < 0:\n                # [-1, 1] -> [0, 1]\n                agentview_img = (agentview_img + 1.0) / 2.0\n            # else: already in [0, 1] range, assume correctly normalized\n        # ===========================================\n\n        # Clamp to ensure valid range\n        agentview_img = agentview_img.clamp(0.0, 1.0)\n\n        # ===== GPU-ACCELERATED RESIZE (aligned with PIL behavior) =====\n        # PyTorch bilinear with align_corners=False approximates PIL's behavior\n        resized_img = F.interpolate(\n            agentview_img,\n            size=(target_size, target_size),\n            mode=\"bilinear\",\n            align_corners=False,\n        )\n        # =============================================================\n\n        # Convert back to [-1, 1] range (to match PIL-based pipeline)\n        resized_img = resized_img * 2.0 - 1.0  # [0, 1] -> [-1, 1]\n\n        # Add num_images dimension: [B, C, 64, 64] -> [B, 1, C, 64, 64]\n        resized_img = resized_img.unsqueeze(1)\n\n        return resized_img\n\n    def _preprocess_states(self, states):\n        \"\"\"\n        Preprocess states: flatten to 2D and convert to bfloat16.\n\n        Args:\n            states: [B, ...] any shape\n\n        Returns:\n            states: [B, state_dim] flattened states as bfloat16\n        \"\"\"\n        if states.dim() > 2:\n            states = states.reshape(states.shape[0], -1)\n        # Convert to bfloat16 to match encoder's dtype\n        if states.dtype != torch.bfloat16:\n            states = states.to(torch.bfloat16)\n        return states\n\n    def enable_torch_compile(\n        self,\n        mode: str = \"max-autotune\",\n    ):\n        if self.torch_compile_enabled:\n            return\n\n        self.paligemma_with_expert.paligemma.model.vision_tower.forward = torch.compile(\n            self.paligemma_with_expert.paligemma.model.vision_tower.forward, mode=mode\n        )\n\n        # NOTE: paligemma.model.language_model and gemma_expert.model share the same LLM backbone.\n        # Enabling cuda graph on both simultaneously causes mysterious crashes (likely due to\n        # tensor aliasing in the shared computation graph). We disable cuda graph for\n        # paligemma.model.language_model since it is not CPU-bound, while gemma_expert.model\n        # benefits more from cuda graph.\n        self.paligemma_with_expert.paligemma.model.language_model.forward = (\n            torch.compile(\n                self.paligemma_with_expert.paligemma.model.language_model.forward,\n                mode=\"max-autotune-no-cudagraphs\" if mode == \"max-autotune\" else mode,\n            )\n        )\n        self.paligemma_with_expert.gemma_expert.model.forward = torch.compile(\n            self.paligemma_with_expert.gemma_expert.model.forward,\n            mode=mode,\n            fullgraph=True,\n        )\n        self.get_logprob_norm = torch.compile(\n            self.get_logprob_norm,\n            mode=\"max-autotune-no-cudagraphs\" if mode == \"max-autotune\" else mode,\n        )\n\n        self.torch_compile_enabled = True\n"}

SOURCE_JSON {"worktree": "pi05-robotwin-rl", "rel": "rlinf/algorithms/advantages.py", "sha256": "a324ac98794fe9fcb1d2df3186ef1865c03cd2b06029aa3b4ffc03ab2059e791", "text": "# Copyright 2025 The RLinf Authors.\n#\n# Licensed under the Apache License, Version 2.0 (the \"License\");\n# you may not use this file except in compliance with the License.\n# You may obtain a copy of the License at\n#\n#     https://www.apache.org/licenses/LICENSE-2.0\n#\n# Unless required by applicable law or agreed to in writing, software\n# distributed under the License is distributed on an \"AS IS\" BASIS,\n# WITHOUT WARRANTIES OR CONDITIONS OF ANY KIND, either express or implied.\n# See the License for the specific language governing permissions and\n# limitations under the License.\n\nfrom typing import Optional\n\nimport torch\n\nfrom rlinf.algorithms.registry import register_advantage\nfrom rlinf.algorithms.utils import kl_penalty, safe_normalize\nfrom rlinf.utils.utils import masked_mean\n\n\n@register_advantage(\"gae\")\ndef compute_gae_advantages_and_returns(\n    rewards: torch.Tensor,\n    gamma: float = 1.0,\n    gae_lambda: float = 1.0,\n    values: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n    normalize_advantages: bool = True,\n    normalize_returns: bool = False,\n    loss_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n    dones: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n    **kwargs,\n) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Calculate advantages and returns for Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO).\n    NOTE: currently this function does not support auto-reset.\n\n    This function implements Generalized Advantage Estimation (GAE) to compute\n    advantages and returns for PPO training. The advantages are normalized\n    using mean and standard deviation for stable training.\n\n    Args:\n        rewards (torch.Tensor): Rewards per timestep. Shape: [seq_len, bsz].\n        values (torch.Tensor): Value function estimates. Shape: [seq_len, bsz].\n        dones (torch.Tensor): Done flags (1 if episode ended, else 0).\n        gamma (float, optional): Discount factor. Defaults to 1.0.\n        gae_lambda (float, optional): GAE smoothing factor. Defaults to 1.0.\n        normalize_advantages (bool, optional): Whether to normalize advantages. Defaults to True.\n        normalize_returns (bool, optional): Whether to normalize returns. Defaults to False.\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]: (advantages, returns)\n    \"\"\"\n    T = rewards.shape[0]\n    advantages = torch.zeros_like(rewards)\n    returns = torch.zeros_like(rewards)\n    gae = 0\n\n    critic_free = values is None\n    if critic_free:\n        gae_lambda = 1\n        gamma = 1\n\n    for step in reversed(range(T)):\n        if critic_free:\n            delta = rewards[step]\n        else:\n            delta = (\n                rewards[step]\n                + gamma * values[step + 1] * (~dones[step + 1])\n                - values[step]\n            )\n\n        gae = delta + gamma * gae_lambda * (~dones[step + 1]) * gae\n        returns[step] = gae if critic_free else gae + values[step]\n\n    advantages = returns - values[:-1] if not critic_free else returns\n\n    if normalize_advantages:\n        advantages = safe_normalize(advantages, loss_mask=loss_mask)\n    if normalize_returns:\n        returns = safe_normalize(returns, loss_mask=loss_mask)\n\n    return advantages, returns\n\n\n@register_advantage(\"grpo\")\ndef compute_grpo_advantages(\n    rewards: torch.Tensor,\n    loss_mask: torch.Tensor,\n    group_size: int,\n    **kwargs,\n):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute GRPO advantages.\n\n    Args:\n        rewards (torch.Tensor): Reward or score values. Shape: [num_groups, group_size]\n        loss_mask (torch.Tensor): Loss mask for valid entries. Shape: [num_groups, group_size]\n        group_size (int): Group size for advantage computation.\n\n    Returns:\n        torch.Tensor: advantages\n    \"\"\"\n    grouped_rewards = rewards.view(-1, group_size)\n\n    grouped_reward_mean = grouped_rewards.mean(dim=-1, keepdim=True).expand_as(\n        grouped_rewards\n    )\n    grouped_reward_std = grouped_rewards.std(dim=-1, keepdim=True).expand_as(\n        grouped_rewards\n    )\n\n    advantages = grouped_rewards - grouped_reward_mean\n    advantages = advantages / (grouped_reward_std + 1e-6)\n\n    advantages = (torch.zeros_like(loss_mask) + advantages.view(1, -1)) * loss_mask\n\n    return advantages, None\n\n\n@register_advantage(\"grpo_video\")\ndef compute_grpo_video_advantages(\n    rewards: torch.Tensor,\n    loss_mask: torch.Tensor,\n    group_size: int,\n    **kwargs,\n):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute fine-grained GRPO advantages for video frame/chunk rewards.\n\n    After embodied preprocessing, action-level video rewards have shape\n    [num_steps, batch_size], where num_steps is usually the number of decoded\n    frame chunks and batch_size is ordered as [num_prompts * group_size]. This\n    function reshapes rewards to [num_steps, num_prompts, group_size] and returns\n    advantages with the same [num_steps, batch_size] shape, so each frame/chunk\n    can receive its own credit signal.\n\n    advantage_mode=\"frame\":\n        Normalize each frame/chunk independently over the group_size samples.\n        Frame 0 only competes with frame 0 from other samples of the same prompt;\n        frame 1 only competes with frame 1, and so on.\n\n    advantage_mode=\"video\":\n        Normalize all frame/chunk rewards within the same prompt group together\n        over dimensions [num_steps, group_size]. This keeps frame-level\n        advantages but uses one video-level mean/std per prompt group.\n    \"\"\"\n    num_steps, batch_size = rewards.shape\n    grouped_rewards = rewards.reshape(num_steps, -1, group_size)\n    advantage_mode = kwargs.get(\"advantage_mode\")\n    if advantage_mode == \"frame\":\n        grouped_reward_mean = grouped_rewards.mean(dim=-1, keepdim=True)\n        grouped_reward_std = grouped_rewards.std(dim=-1, keepdim=True)\n    elif advantage_mode == \"video\":\n        grouped_reward_mean = grouped_rewards.mean(dim=(0, 2), keepdim=True)\n        grouped_reward_std = grouped_rewards.std(dim=(0, 2), keepdim=True)\n    else:\n        raise ValueError(f\"Unsupported grpo_video advantage_mode: {advantage_mode}\")\n    advantages = (grouped_rewards - grouped_reward_mean) / (grouped_reward_std + 1e-6)\n    advantages = advantages.reshape(num_steps, batch_size)\n    return advantages * loss_mask, None\n\n\n@register_advantage(\"grpo_dynamic\")\ndef compute_grpo_dynamic_advantages(\n    rewards: torch.Tensor,\n    loss_mask: torch.Tensor,\n    group_size: int,\n    idx_to_traj: list[int],\n    advantage_mode: str = \"turn\",  # \"trajectory\" or \"turn\"\n    **kwargs,\n):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute GRPO advantages for multi-turn multi-agent scenarios.\n\n    IMPORTANT: This function computes advantages PER QUESTION, not globally.\n    - idx_to_traj maps turn_idx -> global_traj_idx (e.g., [0,0,1,1,2,2,3,3,4,4,...,15,15])\n    - Trajectories 0-3 belong to question 0, 4-7 to question 1, etc.\n    - We must compute GRPO separately for each question's group_size trajectories\n\n    Two advantage computation modes:\n    1. \"trajectory\": Trajectory-level GRPO (Method 1)\n       - Compute mean/std over group_size trajectory rewards per question\n       - Broadcast same advantage to all turns in a trajectory\n       - Example: Q0 has 4 trajs with 1,2,3,4 turns. Compute GRPO over 4 traj rewards,\n                  then assign traj0_adv to its 1 turn, traj1_adv to its 2 turns, etc.\n\n    2. \"turn\": Turn-level GRPO (Method 2)\n       - Compute mean/std over all turns within each question\n       - Example: Q0 has 4 trajs with 1,2,3,4 turns = 10 turns total.\n                  Compute GRPO over these 10 turn rewards (currently all same within traj).\n       - Future-proof: works when turns have different rewards within same trajectory\n\n    Args:\n        rewards: Shape [num_sequence, 1] after preprocessing (num_sequence = total turns)\n        loss_mask: Shape [seq_len, num_sequence] after preprocessing\n        group_size: Number of trajectories per question (e.g., 4)\n        idx_to_traj: List mapping turn_idx -> global_traj_idx\n        advantage_mode: \"trajectory\" or \"turn\"\n\n    Returns:\n        advantages: Shape [seq_len, num_sequence]\n    \"\"\"\n    num_sequence = len(idx_to_traj)\n\n    rewards_flat = rewards.squeeze(-1)\n\n    assert rewards_flat.numel() == num_sequence, (\n        f\"Rewards size mismatch: {rewards_flat.numel()} != {num_sequence}\"\n    )\n\n    num_trajectories = max(idx_to_traj) + 1\n    num_questions = num_trajectories // group_size\n    assert num_trajectories % group_size == 0, (\n        f\"num_trajectories {num_trajectories} not divisible by group_size {group_size}\"\n    )\n\n    turn_advantages = torch.zeros(\n        num_sequence, dtype=rewards.dtype, device=rewards.device\n    )\n\n    if advantage_mode == \"trajectory\":\n        # Aggregate turn rewards into per-trajectory rewards first.\n        trajectory_rewards = torch.zeros(\n            num_trajectories, dtype=rewards.dtype, device=rewards.device\n        )\n        trajectory_counts = torch.zeros(\n            num_trajectories, dtype=torch.long, device=rewards.device\n        )\n\n        for turn_idx, traj_idx in enumerate(idx_to_traj):\n            trajectory_rewards[traj_idx] += rewards_flat[turn_idx]\n            trajectory_counts[traj_idx] += 1\n\n        # Step 1: Average rewards per trajectory.\n        trajectory_rewards = trajectory_rewards / trajectory_counts.clamp(min=1).float()\n\n        # Step 2: reshape to [num_questions, group_size] for per-question GRPO.\n        trajectory_rewards_grouped = trajectory_rewards.view(num_questions, group_size)\n\n        # Step 3: compute per-question mean and std.\n        per_question_mean = trajectory_rewards_grouped.mean(\n            dim=-1, keepdim=True\n        )  # [num_questions, 1]\n        per_question_std = trajectory_rewards_grouped.std(\n            dim=-1, keepdim=True\n        )  # [num_questions, 1]\n\n        # Step 4: normalize within each question group.\n        normalized_trajectory_rewards = (\n            trajectory_rewards_grouped - per_question_mean\n        ) / (per_question_std + 1e-6)  # [num_questions, group_size]\n\n        # Step 5: flatten back to [num_trajectories].\n        normalized_trajectory_rewards = normalized_trajectory_rewards.view(-1)\n\n        # Step 6: broadcast trajectory advantages to all turns in that trajectory.\n        for turn_idx, traj_idx in enumerate(idx_to_traj):\n            turn_advantages[turn_idx] = normalized_trajectory_rewards[traj_idx]\n\n    elif advantage_mode == \"turn\":\n        # Step 1: map each turn to its owning question.\n        turn_to_question = torch.tensor(\n            [idx_to_traj[i] // group_size for i in range(num_sequence)],\n            dtype=torch.long,\n            device=rewards.device,\n        )\n\n        # Step 2: normalize turn rewards within each question group.\n        for question_idx in range(num_questions):\n            question_mask = turn_to_question == question_idx\n            question_turn_rewards = rewards_flat[question_mask]\n\n            # Step 3: compute mean and std for all turns in this question.\n            question_mean = question_turn_rewards.mean()\n            question_std = question_turn_rewards.std()\n\n            # Step 4: normalize turn rewards within the question.\n            normalized_question_rewards = (question_turn_rewards - question_mean) / (\n                question_std + 1e-6\n            )\n\n            # Step 5: write normalized turn-level advantages back.\n            turn_advantages[question_mask] = normalized_question_rewards\n\n    else:\n        raise ValueError(\n            f\"Invalid advantage_mode: {advantage_mode}. Must be 'trajectory' or 'turn'\"\n        )\n\n    advantages = torch.zeros_like(\n        loss_mask, dtype=rewards.dtype\n    ) + turn_advantages.view(1, -1)\n    advantages = advantages * loss_mask\n\n    return advantages, None\n\n\n@register_advantage(\"reinpp\")\ndef compute_reinpp_advantages(\n    rewards: torch.Tensor,\n    loss_mask: torch.Tensor,\n    group_size: int,\n    use_reinpp_baseline: bool = False,\n    kl_beta: float = 0.0,\n    logprob=None,\n    ref_logprob=None,\n    kl_penalty_type: str = \"\",\n    **kwargs,\n):\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute advantages for reinforce++ and reinforce++ baseline.\n\n    Args:\n        rewards (torch.Tensor): The reward or score values.\n        loss_mask (torch.Tensor): The loss mask for valid entries.\n        group_size (int): The group size for advantage computation.\n        use_reinpp_baseline (bool, optional): Whether to use reinforce++ baseline.\n        kl_beta (float, optional): KL penalty coefficient.\n        logprob (optional): Log probability of current policy.\n        ref_logprob (optional): Log probability of reference policy.\n        kl_penalty_type (str, optional): Type of KL penalty.\n\n    Returns:\n        torch.Tensor: advantages\n    \"\"\"\n    # first group baseline for reinforce++ baseline\n    if use_reinpp_baseline:\n        grouped_rewards = rewards.view(-1, group_size)  # [num_prompt, group_size]\n        grouped_rewards -= grouped_rewards.mean(dim=1, keepdims=True)\n        rewards = grouped_rewards.view(-1)  # [B]\n\n    # build the reward matrix\n    r_matrix = torch.zeros_like(loss_mask).float()  # [L, B]\n    seq_length = loss_mask.size(0)\n    mask_flipped = loss_mask.long().fliplr()\n    eos_positions = mask_flipped.argmax(\n        dim=0, keepdim=True\n    )  # position of last True in original mask\n    eos_indices = seq_length - 1 - eos_positions  # [1, B]\n\n    r_matrix = r_matrix.scatter_(dim=0, index=eos_indices, src=rewards)  # [L, B]\n\n    # add kl penalty\n    if kl_beta > 0:\n        kld = kl_penalty(logprob, ref_logprob, kl_penalty=kl_penalty_type)  # [L, B]\n        r_matrix -= kl_beta * kld\n\n    # compute return\n    ret_matrix = torch.cumsum(r_matrix.flip(dims=[0]), dim=0).flip(dims=[0])\n\n    # normalize\n    advantages = ret_matrix.clone()\n\n    mean = masked_mean(advantages, loss_mask)\n    var = masked_mean((advantages - mean).pow(2), loss_mask)\n    rstd = var.clamp(min=1e-8).rsqrt()\n\n    advantages = (advantages - mean) * rstd\n\n    return advantages, None\n\n\n@register_advantage(\"opd\")\ndef compute_opd_advantages(\n    prev_logprobs: torch.Tensor,\n    teacher_logprobs: torch.Tensor,\n    loss_mask: Optional[torch.Tensor] = None,\n    normalize_advantages: bool = False,\n    **kwargs,\n):\n    \"\"\"Compute OPD advantages from frozen teacher token log-probabilities.\"\"\"\n    assert teacher_logprobs is not None, (\n        \"OPD advantage computation requires post-rollout teacher_logprobs.\"\n    )\n    assert prev_logprobs is not None, (\n        \"OPD advantage computation requires prev_logprobs from student rollout.\"\n    )\n    assert teacher_logprobs.shape == prev_logprobs.shape, (\n        f\"teacher_logprobs shape {teacher_logprobs.shape} must match \"\n        f\"prev_logprobs shape {prev_logprobs.shape}.\"\n    )\n    assert not normalize_advantages, (\n        \"VLA-OPD uses raw reverse-KL rewards; set normalize_advantages to False.\"\n    )\n    num_action_chunks = kwargs.get(\"num_action_chunks\", None)\n    assert num_action_chunks is not None, (\n        \"OPD advantage computation requires num_action_chunks.\"\n    )\n    advantages = teacher_logprobs.float() - prev_logprobs.float()\n    assert advantages.shape[-1] % num_action_chunks == 0, (\n        f\"OPD token count {advantages.shape[-1]} must be divisible by \"\n        f\"num_action_chunks {num_action_chunks}.\"\n    )\n    advantages = advantages.reshape(*advantages.shape[:-1], num_action_chunks, -1)\n    if loss_mask is not None:\n        target_steps = loss_mask.shape[0]\n        assert advantages.shape[0] in {target_steps, target_steps + 1}, (\n            f\"OPD advantages time dimension {advantages.shape[0]} must match \"\n            f\"loss_mask time dimension {target_steps} or include one bootstrap step.\"\n        )\n        advantages = advantages[:target_steps]\n\n    return advantages, None\n\n\n@register_advantage(\"raw\")\ndef compute_raw_advantages(\n    rewards: torch.Tensor,\n    loss_mask: torch.Tensor,\n    normalize_advantages: bool = False,\n    **kwargs,\n):\n    \"\"\"\n    Return raw rewards or normalized rewards.\n\n    Args:\n        rewards (torch.Tensor): Reward or score values. Shape: [num_groups, group_size]\n        loss_mask (torch.Tensor): Loss mask for valid entries. Shape: [num_groups, group_size]\n        normalize_advantages (bool): Whether to normalize advantages.\n\n    Returns:\n        torch.Tensor: advantages\n    \"\"\"\n    if rewards.ndim == 2:\n        rewards = rewards.reshape(-1)\n    advantages = rewards.unsqueeze(0).expand_as(loss_mask) * loss_mask\n\n    # Simple baseline subtraction (mean of valid advantages)\n    if normalize_advantages:\n        valid = advantages[loss_mask.bool()]\n        if valid.numel() > 0:\n            advantages = (advantages - valid.mean()) / (valid.std() + 1e-5)\n\n    return advantages, None\n"}

stability\"\n    )\n    assert advantages.shape == logprobs.shape, (\n        f\"OPD advantages shape {advantages.shape} must match logprobs shape \"\n        f\"{logprobs.shape}.\"\n    )\n    assert
